774545 sh -c RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT=400; export RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT rch_scope_launch_source() { command cat <<'RCH_SCOPED_LAUNCH_SOURCE_104' # Process-group identity shared by launch, timeout cleanup, and recovery. # A PID is not durable authority: bind it to the worker boot and leader start. set -f # Fixed printf/stat formats use double quotes: four transport layers multiply # literal apostrophes and must fit the existing 110 KB argument budget.  # Local patch 6: RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT (whole percent, set by the RCH client from RCH_TIMING_SCALE) multiplies the # observation deadlines and settle polls below. Anything but 100..1000 means 100. rch_timing_pct=${RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT:-100} case "$rch_timing_pct" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0*) rch_timing_pct=100;; esac { [ "${#rch_timing_pct}" -le 4 ] && [ "$rch_timing_pct" -ge 100 ] && [ "$rch_timing_pct" -le 1000 ]; } || rch_timing_pct=100  rch_valid_pgid() {     case "$1" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0*|1) return 1;; esac     [ "${#1}" -le 10 ] && [ "$1" -le 2147483647 ] }  rch_valid_boot() {     case "$1" in *[!0-9a-f-]*) return 1;; esac     case "$1" in ????????-????-????-????-????????????) return 0;; esac     return 1 }  # Cheap scheduling hints, never completion evidence: 3s + 1s/2048 tasks + # 1s/16 load units, capped at 8s. Issue #129 gets 7s for a measured 0.7--1.2s # census. Missing/malformed hints use 5s. No census is needed to size a census. rch_observation_budget() {     rch_observation_seconds=5     if read -r rch_load_one rch_load_five rch_load_fifteen rch_load_tasks rch_load_last \         2>/dev/null < /proc/loadavg; then         rch_load_whole=${rch_load_one%%.*}         rch_load_threads=${rch_load_tasks#*/}         case "$rch_load_one:$rch_load_tasks" in *.*:*/*) ;; *) return 0;; esac         case "${rch_load_one#*.}" in [0-9][0-9]) ;; *) return 0;; esac         for rch_load_value in "$rch_load_whole" "$rch_load_threads" "${rch_load_tasks%/*}"; do             case "$rch_load_value" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0[0-9]*) return 0;; esac             [ "${#rch_load_value}" -le 9 ] || return 0         done         rch_observation_seconds=$((3 + rch_load_threads / 2048 + rch_load_whole / 16))         [ "$rch_observation_seconds" -le 8 ] || rch_observation_seconds=8     fi }  # Monotonic hundredths. No recursive rch_control_capture in the Darwin clock; # its one stdlib process has no pipe holders and is independently bounded. rch_observation_clock() {     if [ ! -r /proc/uptime ]; then         [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ] || return 1         rch_observation_now=$(rch_group_timeout --signal=KILL 5s python3 -I -S -c \             'import time; print(time.clock_gettime_ns(time.CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW) // 10000000)') || return 1         case "$rch_observation_now" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0[0-9]*) return 1;; esac         [ "${#rch_observation_now}" -le 15 ]         return $?     fi     read -r rch_observation_uptime rch_observation_idle < /proc/uptime || return 1     case "$rch_observation_uptime" in *.*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_observation_secs=${rch_observation_uptime%%.*}     rch_observation_frac=${rch_observation_uptime#*.}     case "$rch_observation_secs" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0[0-9]*) return 1;; esac     [ "${#rch_observation_secs}" -le 12 ] || return 1     case "$rch_observation_frac" in [0-9][0-9]) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_observation_now=$((rch_observation_secs * 100 + 1$rch_observation_frac - 100)) }  # Owners seed the floor; captures advance their subprocess-local copy. rch_observation_clock_checked() {     rch_observation_clock || return 1     if [ -n "${rch_observation_last_clock:-}" ]; then         rch_scope_uint "$rch_observation_last_clock" || return 1         [ "${#rch_observation_last_clock}" -le 15 ] || return 1         [ "$rch_observation_now" -ge "$rch_observation_last_clock" ] || return 1     fi     rch_observation_last_clock=$rch_observation_now }  # A foreign procfs view proves neither ownership nor absence. NSpid must have # exactly our own ID, not a coincidentally equal first ID in a namespace chain. # Builtin read observes this shell; exec under substitution supplies its PID # even in a subshell where $$ names the parent. Missing evidence fails closed. rch_check_procfs_namespace() {     # Darwin has one PID view. Never infer this from missing Linux procfs:     # Linux must still supply positive namespace evidence at every boundary.     case "$(uname -s)" in Darwin) return 0;; Linux) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_self_pid=$(exec /bin/sh -c 'echo "$PPID"') || return 1     while IFS= read -r rch_status_line; do         case "$rch_status_line" in             NSpid:*)                 set -- ${rch_status_line#NSpid:}                 [ "$#" -eq 1 ] && [ "$1" = "$rch_self_pid" ]                 return $?;;         esac     done < /proc/self/status 2>/dev/null     return 1 }  # Native libproc birth time (microseconds), not rounded ps lstart seconds. # The worker binary is required on Darwin; missing/old helpers fail closed. rch_darwin_process() {     rch_darwin_helper=$(command -v rch-wkr) || return 1     rch_observation_budget     rch_darwin_result=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" "$rch_darwin_helper" process-identity "$1") || return 1     set -- $rch_darwin_result     [ "$#" = 3 ] && rch_valid_boot "$1" && rch_valid_pgid "$3" || return 1     case "$2" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0*) return 1;; esac     [ "${#2}" -le 20 ] || return 1     rch_observed_boot=$1     rch_observed_start=$2     rch_observed_pgid=$3 }  rch_read_boot() {     case "$(uname -s)" in         Linux) rch_check_procfs_namespace && cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id;;         Darwin)             # Boot changes can retire execution without a process census.             # Require a successful, bounded, byte-validated observation BEFORE             # normalization: a pipeline hides sysctl failure, and substitution             # alone can discard NULs from otherwise plausible UUID output.             rch_observation_budget             rch_boot_output=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" /usr/sbin/sysctl -n kern.bootsessionuuid) || return 1             printf "%s\n" "$rch_boot_output" | LC_ALL=C tr "A-F" "a-f";;         *) return 1;;     esac }  # BSD stat uses -f; do not let GNU-only receipt/output checks break recovery. rch_file_size() {     case "$(uname -s)" in         Darwin) stat -f "%z" "$1";;         Linux) stat -c "%s" -- "$1";;         *) return 1;;     esac }  # Read any bound member, including live TIDs behind a zombie leader. Parse # after the final comm delimiter; spaces/parentheses/newlines are legal in comm. # Function positional arguments leave the launcher command arguments intact. rch_read_process() {     rch_valid_pgid "$1" && rch_check_procfs_namespace || return 1     if [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         rch_darwin_process "$1" && [ "$rch_observed_pgid" = "$rch_pgid" ]         return $?     fi     rch_proc_pid=$1     rch_observed_boot=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id 2>/dev/null) || return 1     rch_valid_boot "$rch_observed_boot" || return 1     rch_stat=$(cat "/proc/$rch_proc_pid/stat" 2>/dev/null) || return 1     case "$rch_stat" in "$rch_proc_pid ("*") "*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_stat=${rch_stat##*) }     set -- $rch_stat     [ "$#" -ge 20 ] && [ "$3" = "$rch_pgid" ] || return 1     # Keep the same conservative state grammar as process_identity.rs. An     # unknown future state is not authority to signal a process group.     case "$1" in R|S|D|T|t|K|W|P|I) ;; *) return 1;; esac     shift 19     rch_observed_start=$1     case "$rch_observed_start" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0*) return 1;; esac     [ "${#rch_observed_start}" -le 20 ] }  rch_read_leader() {     rch_read_process "$rch_pgid" }  rch_remote_leader_matches() {     rch_read_leader || return 1     [ "$rch_observed_boot" = "$rch_boot" ] && [ "$rch_observed_start" = "$rch_start" ] }  # Publish before starting any workload. A complete record appears in one rename. # The caller must already be the session/process-group leader created by setsid. rch_remote_record() {     rch_scope_reset     rch_pgid=$$     rch_valid_pgid "$rch_pgid" && rch_read_leader || return 1     rch_boot=$rch_observed_boot     rch_start=$rch_observed_start     # Refuse before publishing execution authority or spawning the payload.     # Scope placement moves this leader, not the workload into a separate tree.     rch_apply_configured_worker_policy || return 125     rch_record_tmp="$1.$rch_pgid"     if [ "$rch_scope_bound" = 1 ]; then         # One atomic record, not an optional sidecar: losing confinement         # evidence must never downgrade recovery to a PGID-only empty claim.         rch_record="RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V2:$2:$rch_boot:$rch_pgid:$rch_start:$rch_scope_uid:$rch_scope_manager:$rch_scope_invocation:$rch_scope_namespace:$rch_scope_root_id:$rch_scope_node_id:$rch_scope_cgroup"     else         rch_record="RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V1:$2:$rch_boot:$rch_pgid:$rch_start"     fi     (umask 077; set -C; printf "%s\n" "$rch_record" > "$rch_record_tmp") || return 1     mv -f "$rch_record_tmp" "$1" }  rch_read_record() {     rch_identified=0     rch_scope_reset     case "$1" in /*) ;; *) return 42;; esac     [ -f "$1" ] && [ ! -L "$1" ] && [ -r "$1" ] || return 42     rch_record_size=$(rch_file_size "$1") || return 42     [ "$rch_record_size" -le 4096 ] || return 42     rch_record=$(cat "$1" 2>/dev/null) || return 42     case "$rch_record" in         RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V2:*)             rch_read_scope_record "$1" "$2"             return $?;;         RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V1:*)             rch_fields=${rch_record#RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V1:}             rch_record_build=${rch_fields%%:*}             [ "$rch_record_build" = "$2" ] || return 43             rch_fields=${rch_fields#*:}             rch_boot=${rch_fields%%:*}             rch_fields=${rch_fields#*:}             rch_pgid=${rch_fields%%:*}             rch_start=${rch_fields#*:}             rch_valid_boot "$rch_boot" || return 42             case "$rch_start" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0*) return 42;; esac             [ "${#rch_start}" -le 20 ] || return 42             [ "$rch_record" = "RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V1:$2:$rch_boot:$rch_pgid:$rch_start" ] || return 42             # Command substitution strips trailing newlines (and some shells             # discard NULs). Require the original bytes to be one complete line.             rch_record_bytes=$(LC_ALL=C wc -c < "$1") || return 42             rch_record_lines=$(LC_ALL=C wc -l < "$1") || return 42             [ "$rch_record_bytes" -eq "$((${#rch_record} + 1))" ] && \                 [ "$rch_record_lines" -eq 1 ] || return 42             rch_identified=1             ;;         *) rch_pgid=$rch_record;;     esac     rch_valid_pgid "$rch_pgid" || return 42 }  # 0: empty; 1: live; 2: unknown. kill -0 cannot separate ESRCH/EPERM or zombies. # A zombie leader can have live LWPs. Keep their TIDs for escalation; incomplete # thread accounting is unknown, never an empty-group proof. rch_group_state() {     rch_check_procfs_namespace || return 2     rch_observation_budget     if [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         # Darwin process zombie state is terminal for the whole task. Linux         # needs the separate LWP census below (a zombie leader can have LWPs).         [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" != 1 ] || return 2         rch_snapshot=$(LC_ALL=C rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" ps -axo pid=,pgid=,stat=) || return 2         printf "%s\n" "$rch_snapshot" | LC_ALL=C awk -v group="$rch_pgid" -v observer="$$" '             NF != 3 || $1 !~ /^[0-9]+$/ || $2 !~ /^[0-9]+$/ || $3 !~ /^[RSDTIUZE][A-Za-z0-9+<>-]*$/ { bad=1; next }             seen_pid[$1]++ { bad=1 }             $1 == observer { seen=1; if ($2 == group) bad=1 }             $2 == group && $3 !~ /^Z/ { live=1 }             END { if (bad || !seen) exit 2; exit live ? 1 : 0 }         '         return $?     fi     # One deadline covers exchanges and pipe holders. Inspect group LWPs only     # when leaders show threads, including zombie leaders.     rch_task_snapshot=$(LC_ALL=C rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" \         /bin/sh -c '             leaders=$(ps -e -o pid= -o lwp= -o pgid= -o s= -o nlwp=) || exit 2             selection=$(printf "%s\n" "$leaders" | awk -v group="$1" -v observer="$2" '\''                 NF != 5 || $1 !~ /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ || $2 != $1 || $3 !~ /^[0-9]+$/ || $4 !~ /^[RSDTtKWPIXZx][A-Za-z0-9+<>-]*$/ || $5 !~ /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ { bad=1; next }                 seen_pid[$1]++ { bad=1 }                 $1 == observer { seen=1; if ($3 == group) bad=1 }                 $3 == group || $1 == observer {                     ids=ids sep $1; sep=","; rows=rows $0 "\n"                 }                 $3 == group && $5 != 1 { threaded=1 }                 $3 == group && ($4 !~ /^[ZXx]/ || $5 != 1) { unsettled=1 }                 END {                     if (bad || !seen) exit 2                     if (threaded) print "RCH_CENSUS_SELECTED:" ids "|" (unsettled+0)                     else { print "RCH_CENSUS_UNSETTLED 0"; printf "%s", rows }                 }             '\'') || exit 2             case "$selection" in                 RCH_CENSUS_SELECTED:*) selection=${selection#RCH_CENSUS_SELECTED:};;                 *) printf "%s\n" "$selection"; exit $?;;             esac             pids=${selection%|*}             [ -n "$pids" ] || exit 2             printf "RCH_CENSUS_UNSETTLED %s\n" "${selection##*|}"             exec ps -L -q "$pids" -o pid= -o lwp= -o pgid= -o stat= -o nlwp=         ' rch-group-census "$rch_pgid" "$$") || return 2     rch_snapshot=$(printf "%s\n" "$rch_task_snapshot" | LC_ALL=C awk -v group="$rch_pgid" -v observer="$$" '         $1 == "RCH_CENSUS_UNSETTLED" {             if (NF != 2 || $2 !~ /^[01]$/ || census_seen++) bad=1             unsettled=$2             next         }         NF != 5 || $1 !~ /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ || $2 !~ /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ || $3 !~ /^[0-9]+$/ || $4 !~ /^[RSDTtKWPIXZx][A-Za-z0-9+<>-]*$/ || $5 !~ /^[1-9][0-9]*$/ { bad = 1; next }         seen_task[$2]++ { bad = 1 }         $2 == observer { seen = 1; if ($3 == group) bad = 1 }         $3 == group {             members[$1]++             if ($1 in expected && expected[$1] != $5) uncertain = 1             expected[$1] = $5             if ($4 !~ /^[ZXx]/) live = 1         }         { print $2, $3, $4 }         END {             if (bad || !seen || !census_seen) exit 2             # Positive liveness needs no complete census. Requiring a stable             # thread count here would prevent cancelling a busy compiler.             if (live) exit 1             # Live tasks can fork outside this PID list and exit between passes.             # Only a later complete census proves absence.             if (unsettled) exit 2             for (pid in members) if (members[pid] != expected[pid]) uncertain = 1             if (uncertain) exit 2             exit 0         }     ')     rch_posix_state=$?     if [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" = 1 ]; then         # setsid/setpgid descendants can outlive this entire process group.         # Require BOTH the thread census and the bound scope to be empty.         rch_scope_operation state         rch_cgroup_state=$?         case "$rch_posix_state:$rch_cgroup_state" in             0:0) return 0;;             0:1|1:0|1:1) return 1;;             *) return 2;;         esac     fi     return "$rch_posix_state" }  # Capture live birth witnesses while the leader still authenticates the group. # They authorize escalation after TERM kills the leader, only in THIS call. rch_capture_group_witnesses() {     rch_witnesses=     rch_witness_identity=     [ "$rch_identified" -eq 1 ] && rch_remote_leader_matches || return 43     # rch_group_state normalized the validated LWP census to ID/group/state.     # Retain live TIDs, not just process leaders, with the same birth fencing.     rch_member_pids=$(printf "%s\n" "$rch_snapshot" | LC_ALL=C awk -v group="$rch_pgid" '         $2 == group && $3 !~ /^[ZXx]/ { print $1 }     ') || return 44     # One timed exchange bounds ALL witnesses using the same protocol. Partial     # output grants no authority: 46 permits freshly authenticated KILL, not TERM.     [ -n "${rch_launch_source:-}" ] || return 46     rch_observation_budget     rch_collected=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" /bin/sh -c '         command eval "$1" || exit 44         rch_boot=$2; rch_pgid=$3; rch_start=$4; shift 4         rch_remote_leader_matches || exit 43         for member in "$@"; do             # The census classified this member as live. An unavailable             # identity is not permission to omit its escalation witness:             # fall back to independently authenticated immediate KILL.             rch_read_process "$member" || exit 46             [ "$rch_observed_boot" = "$rch_boot" ] || exit 46             printf "%s:%s\n" "$member" "$rch_observed_start" || exit 44         done         rch_remote_leader_matches || exit 43     ' rch-witnesses "$rch_launch_source" "$rch_boot" "$rch_pgid" "$rch_start" $rch_member_pids)     case $? in 0) ;; 43) return 43;; *) return 46;; esac     [ -n "$rch_collected" ] || return 46     for rch_witness in $rch_collected; do         case "$rch_witness" in *:*) ;; *) return 46;; esac         rch_valid_pgid "${rch_witness%%:*}" &&             rch_scope_uint "${rch_witness#*:}" && [ "${rch_witness#*:}" != 0 ] || return 46     done     # Do not transfer authority across a leader exit/reuse during collection.     rch_remote_leader_matches || return 43     rch_witnesses=$rch_collected     rch_witness_identity="$rch_boot:$rch_pgid:$rch_start" }  rch_remote_witness_matches() {     [ "${rch_witness_identity:-}" = "$rch_boot:$rch_pgid:$rch_start" ] || return 1     [ -n "${rch_launch_source:-}" ] || return 1     # Bound post-TERM revalidation; failed exchange grants no orphan authority.     rch_observation_budget     rch_matched=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" /bin/sh -c '         command eval "$1" || exit 44         rch_boot=$2; rch_pgid=$3; shift 3         for witness in "$@"; do             if rch_read_process "${witness%%:*}" &&                 [ "$rch_observed_boot" = "$rch_boot" ] &&                 [ "$rch_observed_start" = "${witness#*:}" ]; then                 printf "RCH_WITNESS_MATCH_V1\n"                 exit 0             fi         done         exit 1     ' rch-witnesses "$rch_launch_source" "$rch_boot" "$rch_pgid" $rch_witnesses) || return 1     [ "$rch_matched" = RCH_WITNESS_MATCH_V1 ] }  # Fence PID view, observer group and leader/TERM witness independently of census. rch_posix_signal_authorized() (     rch_requested_signal=$1     [ "${rch_identified:-0}" = 1 ] || exit 43     rch_check_procfs_namespace || exit 44     if [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         rch_self_pid=$(exec /bin/sh -c 'echo "$PPID"') || exit 44         rch_darwin_process "$rch_self_pid" && [ "$rch_observed_pgid" != "$rch_pgid" ] || exit 44         rch_remote_leader_matches && exit 0         [ "$rch_requested_signal" = KILL ] && [ "${rch_term_sent:-0}" = 1 ] &&             rch_remote_witness_matches || exit 43         exit 0     fi     IFS= read -r rch_observer_stat < "/proc/$rch_self_pid/stat" 2>/dev/null || exit 44     case "$rch_observer_stat" in "$rch_self_pid ("*") "*) ;; *) exit 44;; esac     rch_observer_stat=${rch_observer_stat##*) }     set -- $rch_observer_stat     [ "$#" -ge 20 ] && rch_valid_pgid "$3" && [ "$3" != "$rch_pgid" ] || exit 44     rch_remote_leader_matches && exit 0     [ "$rch_requested_signal" = KILL ] && [ "${rch_term_sent:-0}" = 1 ] &&         rch_remote_witness_matches || exit 43 )  # Identity loss/reuse returns43; only TERM from this call grants orphan escalation. rch_signal_group() {     [ "$rch_identified" -eq 1 ] || return 43     if [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" = 1 ]; then         case "$1" in             KILL)                 rch_scope_operation kill                 case $? in 0) return 0;; 3) return 43;; *) return 44;; esac;;             TERM)                 # Preserve graceful TERM for the authenticated group. Detached                 # groups are covered by the subsequent atomic cgroup.kill.                 rch_scope_operation state                 case $? in 0) return 0;; 1) ;; 3) return 43;; *) return 44;; esac                 if rch_remote_leader_matches; then                     kill -TERM -"$rch_pgid" 2>/dev/null || :                 fi                 return 0;;             *) return 43;;         esac     fi     rch_posix_signal_authorized "$1" || return $?     # Revalidate immediately before EACH signal. dash requires no `--` here.     kill -"$1" -"$rch_pgid" 2>/dev/null || : }  # Fresh-channel recovery only: 0 same boot, 1 earlier boot gone, 2 unknown. # No signal, mutation or success claim. A live supervisor cannot substitute a # changed receipt for stopping its still-owned child. rch_recovery_boot_state() (     # Darwin boot observation also uses the bounded control collector.     export rch_control_diskless=1     [ "${rch_identified:-0}" = 1 ] && rch_check_procfs_namespace || exit 2     current=$(rch_read_boot 2>/dev/null) || exit 2     for value in "$rch_boot" "$current"; do         rch_valid_boot "$value" || exit 2         # The legacy shape check permits hyphens within UUID hex groups. That is         # not sufficient evidence to retire execution without a process census.         compact=         while :; do             compact=$compact${value%%-*}             case "$value" in *-*) value=${value#*-};; *) break;; esac         done         [ "${#compact}" = 32 ] || exit 2         case "$compact" in *[!0-9a-f]*|00000000000000000000000000000000) exit 2;; esac     done     [ "$current" != "$rch_boot" ] || exit 0     # Repeat both observations before accepting the discontinuity. A changed     # or inaccessible view is unknown, never a reason to touch a reused PGID.     confirmed=$(rch_read_boot 2>/dev/null) || exit 2     [ "$confirmed" = "$current" ] && rch_check_procfs_namespace || exit 2     exit 1 )  rch_remote_cancel() {     # No authority may leak between calls, even when parsing the next record fails.     rch_witnesses=     rch_witness_identity=     rch_term_sent=0     rch_read_record "$1" "$2" || return $?     if [ "$rch_identified" = 1 ]; then         rch_recovery_boot_state         case $? in             0) ;; # Same boot: retain all existing live birth/scope checks.             1) return 0;; # Only the old execution is gone, not a successful build.             *) return 44;;         esac     fi     rch_remote_cancel_loaded "$3" }  # Only validated in-memory identity grants signal authority. rch_remote_cancel_loaded() (     # Share 30s inside 45s SSH; subshell isolates deadlines and authority.     # A full build disk must not prevent observing that its writers stopped.     # Keep this preference local to cancellation/supervisor cleanup; workers     # without Python retain the existing bounded file-backed control path.     rch_control_diskless=1     # Witness helpers evaluate the protocol in fresh shells; their nested     # Darwin identity observations must use the same storage-independent path.     export rch_control_diskless     rch_observation_clock_checked || return 44     rch_cancel_deadline=$((rch_observation_now + 3000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))     if [ -n "${rch_observation_deadline:-}" ]; then         case "$rch_observation_deadline" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0[0-9]*) return 44;; esac         [ "${#rch_observation_deadline}" -le 15 ] || return 44         [ "$rch_observation_deadline" -ge "$rch_cancel_deadline" ] || \             rch_cancel_deadline=$rch_observation_deadline     fi     rch_observation_deadline=$rch_cancel_deadline     [ "$rch_observation_now" -lt "$rch_observation_deadline" ] || return 44     rch_witnesses=     rch_witness_identity=     rch_term_sent=0     rch_mode=$1     rch_kill_sent=0     # Birth-fenced KILL precedes slow census; completion still needs census.     if [ "$rch_mode" = kill ] && [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" != 1 ] &&         [ "${rch_identified:-0}" = 1 ] && [ "$(uname -s)" = Linux ]; then         rch_kill_status=0         rch_signal_group KILL || rch_kill_status=$?         case "$rch_kill_status" in             0) rch_kill_sent=1;;             43) ;;             *) return "$rch_kill_status";;         esac     fi     rch_group_state     case $? in         0) [ "$rch_kill_sent" = 1 ] || return 0;;         1) ;;         2)             # Unknown proves neither authority nor death; V1 KILL needs a leader.             if [ "$rch_kill_sent" = 0 ]; then                 [ "${rch_identified:-0}" = 1 ] || return 43                 [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" = 1 ] || rch_posix_signal_authorized KILL || return 44             fi             rch_mode=kill;;         *) return 44;;     esac     if [ "$rch_mode" = term ] && [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" != 1 ]; then         rch_capture_group_witnesses         case $? in             0) ;;             46) rch_mode=kill;; # Signal boundary must still authenticate the leader.             *) return $?;;         esac     fi     if [ "$rch_mode" = term ]; then         rch_signal_group TERM || return $?         rch_term_sent=1         sleep 1 || return 44         rch_group_state         case $? in             0) return 0;;             1) ;;             # TERM witnesses fence KILL; post-kill census still proves death.             2) [ "${rch_scope_bound:-0}" = 1 ] || rch_posix_signal_authorized KILL || return 44;;             *) return 44;;         esac     fi     # Authority loss (43) forbids signals; a complete census may prove cleanup.     rch_kill_status=0     if [ "$rch_kill_sent" = 0 ]; then         rch_signal_group KILL || rch_kill_status=$?     fi     case "$rch_kill_status" in 0|43) ;; *) return "$rch_kill_status";; esac     rch_attempt=0     rch_unknown_attempts=0     rch_last_state=2     # Budgeted settling polls; unknown gets at most three attempts.     rch_attempt_limit=$((20 * rch_timing_pct / 100))     while [ "$rch_attempt" -lt "$rch_attempt_limit" ]; do         rch_observation_clock_checked || return 44         [ "$rch_observation_now" -lt "$rch_observation_deadline" ] || return 44         rch_group_state         rch_last_state=$?         # Unknown grants nothing; recheck between-pass exits within this cap.         case $rch_last_state in             0) return 0;;             1) rch_unknown_attempts=0;;             2)                 [ "$rch_kill_status" = 0 ] || return 43                 rch_unknown_attempts=$((rch_unknown_attempts + 1))                 [ "$rch_unknown_attempts" -lt 3 ] || return 44;;             *) return 44;;         esac         rch_attempt=$((rch_attempt + 1))         sleep 0.1 || return 44     done     [ "$rch_kill_status" = 0 ] || return "$rch_kill_status"     [ $rch_last_state != 2 ] || return 44     return 45 )  # Receiver policy confines tracked Linux payloads, not sync/bootstrap/untracked # work (GH #104). Configure ONE root-owned /etc/rch/worker-execution.conf or # SSH-user/root-owned $HOME/.config/rch/worker-execution.conf: #   version=1 #   nice=10 #   systemd_slice=jobs.slice #   systemd_manager=user # All settings are required; missing policy preserves legacy behavior. Unknown, # duplicate, unreadable or unenforced settings refuse setup; never eval policy.  rch_policy_refuse() {     printf "RCH_WORKER_POLICY_REFUSED:%s\n" "$1" >&2     return 125 }  rch_policy_valid_nice() {     case "$1" in 0|[1-9]|1[0-9]|-[1-9]|-1[0-9]|-20) return 0;; esac     return 1 }  rch_policy_valid_slice() {     case "$1" in *.slice) rch_policy_slice_base=${1%.slice};; *) return 1;; esac     [ -n "$rch_policy_slice_base" ] && [ "${#1}" -le 200 ] || return 1     case "$rch_policy_slice_base" in         -*|*-|*--*|*[!a-zA-Z0-9_-]*) return 1;;     esac }  rch_policy_select_file() {     rch_policy_file=     rch_policy_uid=$(id -u) || { rch_policy_refuse uid; return 125; }     rch_policy_system=/etc/rch/worker-execution.conf     rch_policy_user=     case "${HOME:-}" in /*) rch_policy_user="$HOME/.config/rch/worker-execution.conf";; esac     # An inaccessible containing directory is not evidence of absent policy.     for rch_policy_dir in /etc/rch "${HOME:-/}/.config" "${HOME:-/}/.config/rch"; do         if [ -d "$rch_policy_dir" ] && [ ! -x "$rch_policy_dir" ]; then             rch_policy_refuse unreadable_directory; return 125         fi     done     if [ -e "$rch_policy_system" ] || [ -L "$rch_policy_system" ]; then         rch_policy_file=$rch_policy_system         rch_policy_owner=0     fi     if [ -n "$rch_policy_user" ] && { [ -e "$rch_policy_user" ] || [ -L "$rch_policy_user" ]; }; then         [ -z "$rch_policy_file" ] || { rch_policy_refuse multiple_policies; return 125; }         rch_policy_file=$rch_policy_user         rch_policy_owner=$rch_policy_uid     fi }  # Parse the entire bounded, ASCII data file before changing process attributes. # owner is the expected uid; root-owned user policy is also accepted. rch_policy_read_file() {     rch_policy_nice=     rch_policy_slice=     rch_policy_manager=user     rch_policy_seen=':'     [ -f "$1" ] && [ ! -L "$1" ] && [ -r "$1" ] || {         rch_policy_refuse unreadable_file; return 125;     }     rch_policy_stat=$(LC_ALL=C stat -c "%u:%a:%s:%h" -- "$1") || {         rch_policy_refuse file_metadata; return 125;     }     rch_policy_file_uid=${rch_policy_stat%%:*}     rch_policy_stat=${rch_policy_stat#*:}     rch_policy_mode=${rch_policy_stat%%:*}     rch_policy_stat=${rch_policy_stat#*:}     rch_policy_size=${rch_policy_stat%%:*}     rch_policy_links=${rch_policy_stat#*:}     case "$rch_policy_mode" in [0-7][0-7][0-7]) ;; *) rch_policy_refuse file_mode; return 125;; esac     [ "$rch_policy_file_uid" = "$2" ] || [ "$rch_policy_file_uid" = 0 ] || {         rch_policy_refuse file_owner; return 125;     }     [ "$((0$rch_policy_mode & 022))" -eq 0 ] && [ "$rch_policy_links" = 1 ] || {         rch_policy_refuse writable_or_linked_file; return 125;     }     [ "$rch_policy_size" -le 8192 ] || { rch_policy_refuse file_size; return 125; }     # Shell read can discard NULs. Validate bytes before parsing, not afterwards.     LC_ALL=C tr -cd '\11\12\40-\176' < "$1" | cmp -s - "$1" || {         rch_policy_refuse file_encoding; return 125;     }     while IFS= read -r rch_policy_line || [ -n "$rch_policy_line" ]; do         case "$rch_policy_line" in ''|'#'*) continue;; esac         case "$rch_policy_line" in *=*) ;; *) rch_policy_refuse syntax; return 125;; esac         rch_policy_key=${rch_policy_line%%=*}         rch_policy_value=${rch_policy_line#*=}         case "$rch_policy_seen" in *":$rch_policy_key:"*) rch_policy_refuse duplicate_key; return 125;; esac         rch_policy_seen="$rch_policy_seen$rch_policy_key:"         case "$rch_policy_key" in             version) [ "$rch_policy_value" = 1 ] || { rch_policy_refuse version; return 125; };;             nice)                 rch_policy_valid_nice "$rch_policy_value" || { rch_policy_refuse nice; return 125; }                 rch_policy_nice=$rch_policy_value;;             systemd_slice)                 rch_policy_valid_slice "$rch_policy_value" || { rch_policy_refuse slice; return 125; }                 rch_policy_slice=$rch_policy_value;;             systemd_manager)                 case "$rch_policy_value" in user|system) ;; *) rch_policy_refuse manager; return 125;; esac                 rch_policy_manager=$rch_policy_value;;             *) rch_policy_refuse unknown_key; return 125;;         esac     done < "$1"     case "$rch_policy_seen" in *:version:*) ;; *) rch_policy_refuse missing_version; return 125;; esac     [ -n "$rch_policy_nice$rch_policy_slice" ] || { rch_policy_refuse empty_policy; return 125; }     case "$rch_policy_seen" in         *:systemd_manager:*)             [ -n "$rch_policy_slice" ] || { rch_policy_refuse manager_without_slice; return 125; };;     esac }  # Kill helpers AND pipe holders in a private group, never the workload. Prefer # GNU (gnutimeout/gtimeout): uutils timeout kills only the direct child. rch_group_timeout() {     if command -v gnutimeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then         gnutimeout "$@"     elif command -v gtimeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then         gtimeout "$@"     elif command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then         timeout "$@"     else         # The Darwin launcher already uses Python when setsid is unavailable.         # Do not require Homebrew coreutils merely to observe a native identity.         # Keep this monitor alive as its OWN session/group leader. On a deadline         # or interrupted observer, signal that group, never a reaped child PID         # and never the calling process group (which may contain the compiler).         command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 125         # Isolated mode alone still imports installed .pth/sitecustomize hooks.         # This monitor uses only stdlib: do not run unrelated startup code         # before its alarm is armed or let it corrupt control observations.         python3 -I -S -B -c ' import os import signal import subprocess import sys  if len(sys.argv) < 4 or sys.argv[1] != "--signal=KILL" or sys.argv[2] not in tuple(str(n) + "s" for n in range(1, 9)):     sys.exit(125) try:     os.setsid() except OSError:     sys.exit(125) if os.getpgrp() != os.getpid():     sys.exit(125)  def stop_group(signum=None, frame=None):     try:         os.killpg(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)     finally:         os._exit(125)  for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGALRM):     signal.signal(signum, stop_group) # Arm before spawning too: waiting for exec to complete is part of the probe. signal.alarm(int(sys.argv[2][:-1])) try:     child = subprocess.Popen(sys.argv[3:]) except OSError:     sys.exit(125) status = child.wait() signal.alarm(0) sys.exit(status if status >= 0 else 128 - status) ' "$@"     fi }  rch_control_in_budget() {     [ -n "${rch_observation_deadline:-}" ] || return 0     rch_scope_uint "$rch_observation_deadline" || return 125     [ "${#rch_observation_deadline}" -le 15 ] || return 125     rch_observation_clock_checked || return 125     [ "$rch_observation_now" -lt "$rch_observation_deadline" ] || return 125 }  rch_control_budget() {     [ -n "${rch_observation_deadline:-}" ] || return 0     rch_control_in_budget || return 125     rch_control_remaining=$(((rch_observation_deadline - rch_observation_now) / 100))     # Never pass timeout 0: it disables the deadline.     [ "$rch_control_remaining" -gt 0 ] || return 125     [ "$rch_control_remaining" -ge "${rch_control_timeout%s}" ] || \         rch_control_timeout=${rch_control_remaining}s }  # The existing timeout owns the collector, command and inherited pipe holders # as one process group. Never create another session here. Buffer only bounded, # validated bytes in memory, so cancellation needs no space on the build disk. rch_control_capture_memory() {     rch_control_output=$(         rch_group_timeout --signal=KILL "$rch_control_timeout" python3 -I -S -B -c ' import signal import subprocess import sys  limit = int(sys.argv[1]) allowed = b"\t\n" + bytes(range(32, 127)) output = bytearray() invalid = False interrupted = 0 child = None def interrupt(signum, frame):     global interrupted     interrupted = interrupted or signum # Keep timeout alive if only its collector is interrupted. Exec resets these # handlers in the command; it retains its normal signal dispositions. for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):     signal.signal(signum, interrupt) if interrupted:     sys.exit(128 + interrupted) try:     # Same descriptors as the file-backed path, including a pinned scope on fd 8.     child = subprocess.Popen(sys.argv[2:], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, close_fds=False,                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)     while True:         chunk = child.stdout.read(8192)         if not chunk:             break         if not invalid:             if len(output) + len(chunk) > limit or chunk.translate(None, allowed):                 invalid = True                 output.clear()             else:                 output.extend(chunk)         # Even invalid output must drain to EOF before the collector exits.         # Otherwise timeout could exit while an inherited pipe holder lives.     status = child.wait() except OSError:     if child is None:         sys.exit(125)     # An unreadable pipe is not proof that its holders exited. Keep the owned     # timeout alive to stop the complete group instead of abandoning children.     while True:         signal.pause() if interrupted:     sys.exit(128 + interrupted) if invalid:     sys.exit(125) try:     stream = sys.stdout.buffer if status == 0 else sys.stderr.buffer     stream.write(output)     stream.flush() except OSError:     sys.exit(125) sys.exit(128 + interrupted if interrupted else status if status >= 0 else 128 - status) ' "$rch_control_limit" "$@" </dev/null || exit $?         # Preserve every trailing newline across shell command substitution.         printf "."     ) || return $?     rch_control_output=${rch_control_output%.}     rch_control_in_budget || return 125     printf "%s" "$rch_control_output" || return 125     rch_control_in_budget }  rch_control_capture() {     case "$1" in [1-8]s) ;; *) return 125;; esac     rch_control_timeout=$1     shift     # Nested captures and retries consume one admission/cancellation deadline.     rch_control_budget || return 125     rch_control_limit=65536     case "${1:-}:${4:-}" in         ps:*|/bin/sh:rch-group-census) rch_control_limit=1048576;;     esac     if [ "${rch_control_diskless:-0}" = 1 ] && command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then         # Interpreter lookup/setup is also inside the remaining shared budget.         rch_control_budget || return 125         rch_control_capture_memory "$@"         return $?     fi     # Validate raw bytes before substitution discards NUL; keep output private.     rch_control_base=${TMPDIR:-/tmp}     case "$rch_control_base" in /*) ;; *) return 125;; esac     rch_control_dir=$(umask 077; mktemp -d "$rch_control_base/rch-control-output.XXXXXXXXXX") || return 125     rch_control_file=$rch_control_dir/output     rch_control_pipe=$rch_control_dir/pipe     (umask 077; : > "$rch_control_file" && mkfifo -m 600 "$rch_control_pipe") || {         rm -f -- "$rch_control_pipe" "$rch_control_file"         rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :         return 125     }     # Setup consumed time: clamp again before starting the helper.     rch_control_budget || {         rm -f -- "$rch_control_pipe" "$rch_control_file"         rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :         return 125     }     rch_control_status=0     rch_group_timeout --signal=KILL "$rch_control_timeout" /bin/sh -c '         output=$1         pipe=$2         shift 2         cat -- "$pipe" > "$output" &         reader=$!         "$@" > "$pipe" 2>&1 &         command=$!         wait "$command"; status=$?         wait "$reader" || exit 125         exit "$status"     ' rch-control "$rch_control_file" "$rch_control_pipe" "$@" </dev/null || rch_control_status=$?      rm -f -- "$rch_control_pipe"      # Balance case-pattern parentheses for the Darwin bash 3.2 parser.     rch_control_metadata=$(         case "$(uname -s)" in             (Darwin) LC_ALL=C stat -f "%Lp:%l:%z" "$rch_control_file";;             (Linux) LC_ALL=C stat -c "%a:%h:%s" -- "$rch_control_file";;             (*) exit 125;;         esac     ) || {         rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"         rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :         return 125     }     case "$rch_control_metadata" in         600:1:*) ;;         *) rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"; rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :; return 125;;     esac     rch_control_size=${rch_control_metadata##*:}     case "$rch_control_size" in         ''|*[!0-9]*) rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"; rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :; return 125;;     esac     if [ "$rch_control_size" -gt "$rch_control_limit" ] ||         ! LC_ALL=C tr -cd '\11\12\40-\176' < "$rch_control_file" | cmp -s - "$rch_control_file"; then         rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"         rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :         return 125     fi      if [ "$rch_control_status" = 0 ] && ! rch_control_in_budget; then         rch_control_status=125     fi     if [ "$rch_control_status" -ne 0 ]; then         cat -- "$rch_control_file" >&2         rch_control_output_status=$?         rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"         rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :         [ "$rch_control_output_status" -eq 0 ] || return 125         return "$rch_control_status"     fi     cat -- "$rch_control_file"     rch_control_output_status=$?     rm -f -- "$rch_control_file"     rmdir -- "$rch_control_dir" 2>/dev/null || :     [ "$rch_control_output_status" -eq 0 ] || return 125     # Validation and publication consume the budget too.     rch_control_in_budget }  rch_policy_systemctl() {     rch_control_capture 5s systemctl "--$rch_policy_manager" --no-pager --no-ask-password "$@" }  # Publication may precede a failed final deadline check. Require status AND value. rch_policy_property_is() {     rch_policy_value=$(rch_policy_systemctl show --value --property="$1" -- "$2") &&         [ "$rch_policy_value" = "$3" ] }  # Read the actual launcher unified cgroup. A successful D-Bus job submission # is not evidence that systemd moved it; cgroup v1/ambiguous evidence is refused. rch_policy_current_cgroup() (     rch_policy_cgroup_count=0     rch_policy_cgroup_value=     while IFS= read -r rch_policy_cgroup_line; do         case "$rch_policy_cgroup_line" in             0::/*)                 rch_policy_cgroup_count=$((rch_policy_cgroup_count + 1))                 rch_policy_cgroup_value=${rch_policy_cgroup_line#0::};;         esac     done < "/proc/$$/cgroup"     [ "$rch_policy_cgroup_count" -eq 1 ] || exit 125     printf "%s\n" "$rch_policy_cgroup_value" )  rch_policy_enter_scope() {     command -v busctl >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v systemctl >/dev/null 2>&1 &&         command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1 || { rch_policy_refuse systemd_tools; return 125; }     # An operator must start/configure the selected slice. Do not synthesize a     # missing slice and accidentally run without its intended resource limits.     rch_policy_property_is ActiveState "$rch_policy_slice" active || {         rch_policy_refuse slice_not_active; return 125;     }     rch_policy_slice_cgroup=$(rch_policy_systemctl show --value --property=ControlGroup -- "$rch_policy_slice") || {         rch_policy_refuse slice_cgroup; return 125;     }     case "$rch_policy_slice_cgroup" in /*) ;; *) rch_policy_refuse slice_cgroup; return 125;; esac     [ "$rch_policy_slice_cgroup" != / ] || { rch_policy_refuse root_slice; return 125; }     rch_policy_scope="rch-$rch_boot-$rch_pgid-$rch_start.scope"     # Attach the EXISTING setsid leader, rather than starting an independent     # transient service. All subsequently spawned work keeps the same PGID and     # stdio, so the existing watchdog, cancellation and recovery remain owners.     rch_control_capture 5s busctl "--$rch_policy_manager" --timeout=5s --allow-interactive-authorization=no \         call org.freedesktop.systemd1 /org/freedesktop/systemd1 \         org.freedesktop.systemd1.Manager StartTransientUnit 'ssa(sv)a(sa(sv))' \         "$rch_policy_scope" fail 3 PIDs au 1 "$rch_pgid" \         Slice s "$rch_policy_slice" Description s 'RCH tracked build' 0 >/dev/null || {         rch_policy_refuse scope_creation; return 125;     }     rch_policy_expected_cgroup="$rch_policy_slice_cgroup/$rch_policy_scope"     rch_policy_poll=0     while [ "$(rch_policy_current_cgroup)" != "$rch_policy_expected_cgroup" ]; do         rch_policy_poll=$((rch_policy_poll + 1))         [ "$rch_policy_poll" -lt 50 ] || { rch_policy_refuse scope_not_enforced; return 125; }         sleep 0.1 || { rch_policy_refuse scope_wait; return 125; }     done     rch_policy_property_is ActiveState "$rch_policy_scope" active &&         rch_policy_property_is Slice "$rch_policy_scope" "$rch_policy_slice" &&         rch_policy_property_is ControlGroup "$rch_policy_scope" "$rch_policy_expected_cgroup" || {         rch_policy_refuse scope_evidence; return 125;     }     # Admission includes the ability to stop and observe the whole subtree,     # not merely successful placement. Probe without writing cgroup.kill.     rch_scope_bind_created || { rch_scope_reset; rch_policy_refuse scope_cleanup_prerequisites; return 125; } }  rch_policy_enforce() {     rch_scope_reset     [ "$(uname -s)" = Linux ] || { rch_policy_refuse platform; return 125; }     rch_remote_leader_matches || { rch_policy_refuse process_identity; return 125; }     if [ -n "$rch_policy_nice" ]; then         # util-linux -n becomes relative with POSIXLY_CORRECT. Clear that flag         # only in the helper process; leave the build environment unchanged.         (unset POSIXLY_CORRECT; rch_control_capture 5s renice -n "$rch_policy_nice" -p "$rch_pgid") >/dev/null || {             rch_policy_refuse priority_change; return 125;         }         rch_observation_budget         rch_policy_observed_nice=$(LC_ALL=C rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" \             ps -o ni= -p "$rch_pgid") || {             rch_policy_refuse priority_observation; return 125;         }         # ps pads the numeric field; splitting is safe after the numeric check.         case "$rch_policy_observed_nice" in *[!\ 0-9-]*) rch_policy_refuse priority_observation; return 125;; esac         set -- $rch_policy_observed_nice         [ "$#" -eq 1 ] && [ "$1" = "$rch_policy_nice" ] || {             rch_policy_refuse priority_not_enforced; return 125;         }     fi     rch_policy_scope=     if [ -n "$rch_policy_slice" ]; then         rch_policy_enter_scope || return 125     fi     rch_remote_leader_matches || { rch_policy_refuse process_identity; return 125; }     printf "RCH_WORKER_EXECUTION_POLICY:nice=%s,slice=%s,manager=%s,scope=%s\n" \         "${rch_policy_nice:-inherit}" "${rch_policy_slice:-none}" \         "$rch_policy_manager" "${rch_policy_scope:-none}" >&2 }  rch_apply_configured_worker_policy() {     rch_policy_select_file || return 125     [ -n "$rch_policy_file" ] || return 0     rch_policy_read_file "$rch_policy_file" "$rch_policy_owner" || return 125     rch_policy_enforce }  # Scope recovery (GH #104) fences boot/birth, InvocationID and a pinned inode. # Never signal by unit name; open cgroup.kill through the pinned directory. # Unified cgroups and writable cgroup.kill (Linux 5.14+) are required BEFORE # payload admission. Privileged self-migration is outside this containment.  rch_scope_reset() {     rch_scope_bound=0     rch_scope_uid= rch_scope_manager= rch_scope_invocation=     rch_scope_namespace= rch_scope_root_id= rch_scope_node_id=     rch_scope_cgroup= rch_scope_unit= }  rch_scope_uint() {     case "$1" in ''|*[!0-9]*) return 1;; 0) return 0;; 0*) return 1;; esac     [ "${#1}" -le 20 ] }  rch_scope_inode_id() {     case "$1" in *.*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_scope_uint "${1%%.*}" && rch_scope_uint "${1#*.}" }  rch_scope_binding_valid() {     rch_valid_boot "$rch_boot" && rch_valid_pgid "$rch_pgid" &&         rch_scope_uint "$rch_start" && [ "$rch_start" != 0 ] &&         rch_scope_uint "$rch_scope_uid" && rch_scope_uint "$rch_scope_namespace" &&         rch_scope_inode_id "$rch_scope_root_id" && rch_scope_inode_id "$rch_scope_node_id" || return 1     case "$rch_scope_manager" in user|system) ;; *) return 1;; esac     [ "${#rch_scope_invocation}" = 32 ] || return 1     case "$rch_scope_invocation" in *[!0-9a-f]*|00000000000000000000000000000000) return 1;; esac     [ "${#rch_scope_cgroup}" -le 2048 ] || return 1     case "$rch_scope_cgroup" in         /*) ;; *) return 1;;     esac     case "$rch_scope_cgroup" in         *[!a-zA-Z0-9_@./\\-]*|*//*|*/./*|*/../*|*/.|*/..|*/) return 1;;     esac     rch_scope_unit="rch-$rch_boot-$rch_pgid-$rch_start.scope"     [ "${rch_scope_cgroup##*/}" = "$rch_scope_unit" ] }  rch_read_scope_record() {     IFS=: read -r rch_version rch_record_build rch_boot rch_pgid rch_start \         rch_scope_uid rch_scope_manager rch_scope_invocation rch_scope_namespace \         rch_scope_root_id rch_scope_node_id rch_scope_cgroup <<EOF_RECORD $rch_record EOF_RECORD     [ "$rch_version" = RCH_REMOTE_PROCESS_V2 ] || return 42     [ "$rch_record_build" = "$2" ] || return 43     rch_scope_binding_valid || return 42     rch_record_bytes=$(LC_ALL=C wc -c < "$1") || return 42     rch_record_lines=$(LC_ALL=C wc -l < "$1") || return 42     [ "$rch_record_bytes" -eq "$((${#rch_record} + 1))" ] &&         [ "$rch_record_lines" -eq 1 ] || return 42     # New records are private, single-link receipts created by the SSH user.     # Do not treat an imported or externally writable scope name as authority.     [ "$(LC_ALL=C stat -c "%u:%a:%h" -- "$1")" = "$rch_scope_uid:600:1" ] || return 42     rch_identified=1     rch_scope_bound=1 }  rch_scope_root() {     printf "%s\n" /sys/fs/cgroup }  # One manager snapshot, with a bounded wait. Missing/duplicate fields and # unknown states are unavailable evidence, not an inactive scope. rch_scope_snapshot() {     rch_observation_budget     rch_scope_properties=$(LC_ALL=C rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" systemctl "--$rch_scope_manager" \         --no-pager --no-ask-password show --all --property=LoadState \         --property=ActiveState --property=InvocationID --property=ControlGroup \         -- "$rch_scope_unit") || return 2     rch_scope_load= rch_scope_active= rch_scope_observed_invocation= rch_scope_observed_cgroup=     rch_scope_seen=:     while IFS= read -r rch_scope_property; do         case "$rch_scope_property" in *=*) ;; *) return 2;; esac         rch_scope_key=${rch_scope_property%%=*}         rch_scope_value=${rch_scope_property#*=}         case "$rch_scope_seen" in *":$rch_scope_key:"*) return 2;; esac         rch_scope_seen="$rch_scope_seen$rch_scope_key:"         case "$rch_scope_key" in             LoadState) rch_scope_load=$rch_scope_value;;             ActiveState) rch_scope_active=$rch_scope_value;;             InvocationID) rch_scope_observed_invocation=$rch_scope_value;;             ControlGroup) rch_scope_observed_cgroup=$rch_scope_value;;             *) return 2;;         esac     done <<EOF_PROPERTIES $rch_scope_properties EOF_PROPERTIES     for rch_scope_key in LoadState ActiveState InvocationID ControlGroup; do         case "$rch_scope_seen" in *":$rch_scope_key:"*) ;; *) return 2;; esac     done     case "$rch_scope_load:$rch_scope_active" in         loaded:active|loaded:deactivating|loaded:inactive|loaded:failed|not-found:inactive) ;;         *) return 2;;     esac }  rch_scope_same_view() {     rch_check_procfs_namespace &&         [ "$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id)" = "$rch_boot" ] &&         [ "$(id -u)" = "$rch_scope_uid" ] &&         [ "$(stat -Lc "%i" /proc/self/ns/cgroup)" = "$rch_scope_namespace" ] &&         [ "$(stat -c "%d.%i" -- "$rch_scope_mount")" = "$rch_scope_root_id" ] &&         [ "$(stat -fc "%T" -- "$rch_scope_mount")" = cgroup2fs ] &&         [ "$(CDPATH= cd -- "$rch_scope_mount" && pwd -P)" = "$rch_scope_mount" ] }  # 0: proved absent from a searchable hierarchy; 1: present; 2: unknown. # A failed stat/test under an inaccessible ancestor is never absence evidence. rch_scope_path_state() {     rch_scope_walk=$rch_scope_mount     rch_scope_rest=${rch_scope_cgroup#/}     while [ -n "$rch_scope_rest" ]; do         [ -d "$rch_scope_walk" ] && [ ! -L "$rch_scope_walk" ] &&             [ -r "$rch_scope_walk" ] && [ -x "$rch_scope_walk" ] || return 2         rch_scope_walk="$rch_scope_walk/${rch_scope_rest%%/*}"         if [ ! -e "$rch_scope_walk" ] && [ ! -L "$rch_scope_walk" ]; then return 0; fi         case "$rch_scope_rest" in             */*) rch_scope_rest=${rch_scope_rest#*/};;             *) rch_scope_rest=;;         esac     done     [ -d "$rch_scope_walk" ] && [ ! -L "$rch_scope_walk" ] &&         [ -r "$rch_scope_walk" ] && [ -x "$rch_scope_walk" ] || return 2     return 1 }  rch_scope_snapshot_matches() {     [ "$rch_scope_load" = loaded ] &&         [ "$rch_scope_observed_invocation" = "$rch_scope_invocation" ] &&         [ "$rch_scope_observed_cgroup" = "$rch_scope_cgroup" ] }  # Operations use subshell-private descriptors so caller lease/stream FDs are # untouched. 0: successful probe/kill or empty state; 1: populated state; # 2: unavailable evidence; 3: changed identity. kill success is NOT death proof. rch_scope_operation() (     rch_scope_binding_valid || exit 3     rch_scope_mount=$(rch_scope_root) || exit 2     rch_scope_same_view || exit 3     rch_scope_snapshot || exit 2     rch_scope_path_state     case $? in         0)             # Scope GC may already have removed its unit. Require BOTH a             # terminal manager snapshot and physical absence in the same view.             [ "$1" != probe ] || exit 2             case "$rch_scope_load:$rch_scope_active" in                 not-found:inactive)                     case "$rch_scope_observed_invocation" in ''|00000000000000000000000000000000) ;; *) exit 3;; esac;;                 loaded:inactive|loaded:failed)                     [ "$rch_scope_observed_invocation" = "$rch_scope_invocation" ] || exit 3;;                 *) exit 2;;             esac             [ -z "$rch_scope_observed_cgroup" ] || exit 2             rch_scope_same_view || exit 3             exit 0;;         1) ;;         *) exit 2;;     esac     rch_scope_snapshot_matches || exit 3     exec 8<"$rch_scope_mount$rch_scope_cgroup" || exit 2     [ "$(stat -Lc "%d.%i" /proc/self/fd/8)" = "$rch_scope_node_id" ] &&         [ "$(stat -fLc "%T" /proc/self/fd/8)" = cgroup2fs ] || exit 3     # Repeat generation evidence AFTER pinning the inode. Holding this directory     # prevents inode reuse; subsequent opens never resolve its pathname again.     rch_scope_snapshot || exit 2     rch_scope_snapshot_matches || exit 3     case "$1" in         state)             LC_ALL=C awk '                 $1 == "populated" {                     n++; if (NF != 2 || ($2 != "0" && $2 != "1")) bad=1                     value=$2                 }                 END { if (bad || n != 1) exit 2; exit value }             ' /proc/self/fd/8/cgroup.events             exit $?;;         probe|kill)             [ "$(cat /proc/self/fd/8/cgroup.type)" = domain ] || exit 2             # Opening proves capability/permission without killing the launcher             # during admission. The kernel file atomically kills all descendant             # cgroups, including fork races and processes which called setsid.             [ -f /proc/self/fd/8/cgroup.kill ] || exit 2             exec 7>/proc/self/fd/8/cgroup.kill || exit 2             if [ "$1" = kill ]; then printf "1\n" >&7 || exit 2; fi             exit 0;;         *) exit 2;;     esac )  rch_scope_bind_created() {     rch_scope_uid=$(id -u) || return 1     rch_scope_manager=$rch_policy_manager     rch_scope_cgroup=$rch_policy_expected_cgroup     rch_scope_invocation=$(rch_policy_systemctl show --value --property=InvocationID -- "$rch_policy_scope") || return 1     rch_scope_namespace=$(stat -Lc "%i" /proc/self/ns/cgroup) || return 1     rch_scope_mount=$(rch_scope_root) || return 1     rch_scope_root_id=$(stat -c "%d.%i" -- "$rch_scope_mount") || return 1     rch_scope_node_id=$(stat -c "%d.%i" -- "$rch_scope_mount$rch_scope_cgroup") || return 1     rch_scope_binding_valid || return 1     # Retry observations of this same binding, never scope creation or a     # changed identity. Keep the payload gate closed throughout the retries.     (         rch_observation_clock_checked || exit 1         rch_probe_deadline=$((rch_observation_now + 3000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))         if [ -n "${rch_observation_deadline:-}" ]; then             case "$rch_observation_deadline" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0[0-9]*) exit 1;; esac             [ "${#rch_observation_deadline}" -le 15 ] || exit 1             [ "$rch_observation_deadline" -ge "$rch_probe_deadline" ] || \                 rch_probe_deadline=$rch_observation_deadline         fi         rch_observation_deadline=$rch_probe_deadline         rch_probe_attempt=0         while [ "$rch_probe_attempt" -lt 3 ]; do             rch_control_in_budget || exit 1             rch_scope_operation probe             case $? in 0) exit 0;; 2) ;; *) exit 1;; esac             rch_probe_attempt=$((rch_probe_attempt + 1))             sleep 0.1 || exit 1         done         exit 1     ) || return 1     rch_scope_bound=1 }  # Owned supervisor: V1 covers the group; V2 also covers detached descendants. # Completion requires proven cleanup. Keep comments compact: this source is # quoted through four transport layers within the Linux argument-size limit.  # Preserve the open input description across dash asynchronous redirection. # Borrow only a free FD; never reopen paths or overwrite inherited authority. rch_scope_launch_fds() {     __sl_open_fds=     case "$(uname -s)" in         Linux)             rch_check_procfs_namespace || return 1             __sl_fd_dir=/proc/$rch_self_pid/fd             [ -r "$__sl_fd_dir" ] && [ -x "$__sl_fd_dir" ] || return 1             for __sl_fd in 0 2 4 5 6 7 8 9; do                 if [ -e "$__sl_fd_dir/$__sl_fd" ] || [ -L "$__sl_fd_dir/$__sl_fd" ]; then                     __sl_open_fds="$__sl_open_fds $__sl_fd"                 fi             done;;         Darwin)             # No substitution: its pipe would change the FD set. Encode input,             # stderr and a free slot in status 64..94, outside startup failures.             __sl_fd_probe=0             python3 -I -S -c ' import errno, os, sys def opened(fd):     try:         os.fstat(fd)         return True     except OSError as error:         if error.errno != errno.EBADF:             raise         return False state = 64 + 8 * opened(0) + 16 * opened(2) for fd in range(4, 10):     if not opened(fd):         state += fd - 3         break sys.exit(state) ' || __sl_fd_probe=$?             [ "$__sl_fd_probe" -ge 64 ] && [ "$__sl_fd_probe" -le 94 ] || return 1             __sl_free_fd=$((__sl_fd_probe & 7))             [ "$__sl_free_fd" -le 6 ] || return 1             [ "$((__sl_fd_probe & 8))" = 0 ] || __sl_open_fds=0             [ "$((__sl_fd_probe & 16))" = 0 ] || __sl_open_fds="$__sl_open_fds 2"             # Conservatively reserve all other slots; only one is borrowed.             for __sl_fd in 4 5 6 7 8 9; do                 [ "$__sl_fd" = "$((__sl_free_fd + 3))" ] || __sl_open_fds="$__sl_open_fds $__sl_fd"             done;;         *) return 1;;     esac }  rch_scope_launch_fd_open() {     case " $__sl_open_fds " in *" $1 "*) return 0;; esac     return 1 }  rch_scope_launch_save_input() {     __sl_input= __sl_input_redirect=     rch_scope_launch_fds || return 1     if ! rch_scope_launch_fd_open 0; then         __sl_input_redirect='0<&-'         return 0     fi     for __sl_input_candidate in 4 5 6 7 8 9; do         if ! rch_scope_launch_fd_open "$__sl_input_candidate"; then             __sl_input=$__sl_input_candidate             # Only this validated single digit enters eval as program text.             eval "exec $__sl_input<&0" || return 1             __sl_input_redirect="0<&$__sl_input $__sl_input<&-"             return 0         fi     done     return 1 }  rch_scope_launch_close_input() {     case "$__sl_input" in         [4-9]) eval "exec $__sl_input<&-" || return 1;;         '') return 0;;         *) return 1;;     esac     __sl_input= }  # Snapshot policy once; changes must not downgrade a requested scope to V1. rch_scope_launch_select() {     rch_launch_nice= rch_launch_slice= rch_launch_manager=user     rch_policy_select_file || return 125     if [ -n "$rch_policy_file" ]; then         rch_policy_read_file "$rch_policy_file" "$rch_policy_owner" || return 125         rch_launch_nice=$rch_policy_nice         rch_launch_slice=$rch_policy_slice         rch_launch_manager=$rch_policy_manager     fi     rch_apply_configured_worker_policy() {         rch_policy_nice=$rch_launch_nice         rch_policy_slice=$rch_launch_slice         rch_policy_manager=$rch_launch_manager         [ -n "$rch_policy_nice$rch_policy_slice" ] || return 0         rch_policy_enforce     } }  # Cheap liveness of a direct child or the captured supervisor. The namespace is # fenced once before launch. Birth identity is rechecked before EVERY signal. # 0: live, 1: absent/dead, 2: unavailable. No kill -0 == dead inference. rch_scope_launch_process() {     rch_launch_group= rch_launch_birth=     rch_valid_pgid "$1" || return 2     if [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         if ! rch_darwin_process "$1"; then             # Only independent ESRCH proves this process absent after libproc             # failure. This never proves group absence or authorizes signals.             rch_observation_budget             rch_launch_absence=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" python3 -I -S -c ' import errno, os, sys try:     os.getpgid(int(sys.argv[1])) except OSError as error:     if error.errno == errno.ESRCH:         print("RCH_PROCESS_ABSENT_V1:" + sys.argv[1])         sys.exit(0)     raise sys.exit(1) ' "$1") || return 2             [ "$rch_launch_absence" = "RCH_PROCESS_ABSENT_V1:$1" ] && return 1             return 2         fi         rch_launch_group=$rch_observed_pgid         rch_launch_birth=$rch_observed_start         return 0     fi     [ -d "/proc/$1" ] || return 1     IFS= read -r rch_launch_stat < "/proc/$1/stat" 2>/dev/null || return 2     case "$rch_launch_stat" in "$1 ("*") "*) ;; *) return 2;; esac     rch_launch_stat=${rch_launch_stat##*) }     set -- $rch_launch_stat     [ "$#" -ge 20 ] || return 2     case "$1" in Z|X|x) return 1;; R|S|D|T|t|K|W|P|I) ;; *) return 2;; esac     rch_launch_group=$3     shift 19     rch_launch_birth=$1     rch_scope_uint "$rch_launch_birth" && [ "$rch_launch_birth" != 0 ] || return 2 }  # Linux uptime is monotonic, including suspend. Keep its hundredths so a cap # does not expire early because its starting fractional second was discarded. rch_scope_launch_clock() {     if [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         # Darwin continuous time includes suspend; Python monotonic does not.         rch_observation_budget         rch_launch_now=$(rch_control_capture "${rch_observation_seconds}s" python3 -I -S -c \             'import time; print(time.clock_gettime_ns(time.CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW) // 10000000)') || return 1         rch_scope_uint "$rch_launch_now" && [ "${#rch_launch_now}" -le 15 ]         return $?     fi     read -r rch_launch_uptime rch_launch_idle < /proc/uptime || return 1     case "$rch_launch_uptime" in *.*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_launch_secs=${rch_launch_uptime%%.*}     rch_launch_frac=${rch_launch_uptime#*.}     rch_scope_uint "$rch_launch_secs" && [ "${#rch_launch_secs}" -le 12 ] || return 1     case "$rch_launch_frac" in [0-9][0-9]) ;; *) return 1;; esac     rch_launch_now=$((rch_launch_secs * 100 + 1$rch_launch_frac - 100)) }  rch_scope_launch_parent_alive() {     rch_scope_launch_process "$__sl_parent" && [ "$rch_launch_birth" = "$__sl_parent_start" ] }  # Bound marker delivery. Map fd 3 before Python closes nonstandard descriptors. rch_scope_launch_deadline_marker() {     rch_group_timeout --signal=KILL 1s /bin/sh -c 'printf "\n%s\n" "$1"' rch-deadline "$1" \         >&3 2>/dev/null </dev/null }  # Bound diagnostics without changing receipt, claim, status or cleanup verdict. rch_scope_launch_message() (     rch_group_timeout --signal=KILL 1s /bin/sh -c '         case "$2" in             line) printf "%s\n" "$1";;             setup) printf "\n%s_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE\n" "$1";;             *) exit 125;;         esac     ' rch-launch-message "$1" "${2:-line}" </dev/null ) 2>/dev/null  # Policy selects the required authority class. Never accept a V1 downgrade of # a requested scope, or silently assume that an ordinary group has V2 rights. rch_scope_launch_binding_matches() {     if [ -n "$rch_launch_slice" ]; then         [ "$rch_scope_bound" = 1 ]     else         [ "$rch_scope_bound" = 0 ]     fi }  # In-session watchdog membership pins its own group even after leader death. # Historical receipts still need outside birth-fenced cancellation and census. rch_scope_launch_kill_self() {     if [ "$rch_scope_bound" = 1 ]; then         rch_scope_operation kill     elif [ "$(uname -s)" = Darwin ]; then         # Kernel membership grants this in-session authority, never a receipt.         rch_valid_pgid "$rch_pgid" || return 1         python3 -I -S -c ' import os, signal, sys group = os.getpgrp() if group <= 1 or group != int(sys.argv[1]):     sys.exit(1) os.killpg(group, signal.SIGKILL) ' "$rch_pgid"     else         rch_check_procfs_namespace && rch_read_process "$rch_self_pid" &&             [ "$rch_observed_boot" = "$rch_boot" ] || return 1         kill -KILL -"$rch_pgid"     fi }  # Called in a new setsid shell. No payload is allowed until the outside # supervisor validates the selected record class and opens the private gate. rch_scope_launch_child() {     __sl_record=$1; __sl_control=$2; __sl_marker=$3; __sl_build=$4     __sl_parent=$5; __sl_parent_start=$6; __sl_limit=$7     rch_launch_nice=$8; rch_launch_slice=$9; shift 9     rch_launch_manager=$1; shift     [ -z "$rch_launch_nice" ] || rch_policy_valid_nice "$rch_launch_nice" || exit 125     [ -z "$rch_launch_slice" ] || rch_policy_valid_slice "$rch_launch_slice" || exit 125     case "$rch_launch_manager" in user|system) ;; *) exit 125;; esac     rch_apply_configured_worker_policy() {         rch_policy_nice=$rch_launch_nice         rch_policy_slice=$rch_launch_slice         rch_policy_manager=$rch_launch_manager         [ -n "$rch_policy_nice$rch_policy_slice" ] || return 0         rch_policy_enforce     }     rch_observation_clock_checked || exit 125     rch_observation_deadline=$((rch_observation_now + 6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))     rch_remote_record "$__sl_record" "$__sl_build" || exit 125     rch_scope_launch_binding_matches || exit 125     unset rch_observation_deadline rch_observation_last_clock     __sl_cancelled=0     trap '__sl_cancelled=1' HUP INT TERM     while [ ! -f "$__sl_control/go" ]; do         if [ "$__sl_cancelled" = 1 ] || ! rch_scope_launch_parent_alive; then             rch_scope_launch_kill_self             exit 255         fi         sleep 0.1 || exit 255     done     rch_scope_launch_clock || exit 255     __sl_started=$rch_launch_now     # Watchdog survives supervisor loss and remains armed after payload exit     # until proven cleanup. Only V2 covers detached descendants.     (         trap '' HUP INT TERM         while :; do             # This member must stop its pinned group after leader loss, even             # with a live supervisor and no configured build deadline.             if ! rch_scope_launch_parent_alive || ! rch_remote_leader_matches; then                 rch_scope_launch_kill_self                 exit 255             fi             if ! rch_scope_launch_clock; then                 rch_scope_launch_kill_self                 exit 255             fi             if [ "$__sl_limit" -gt 0 ] && [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_started))" -ge "$((__sl_limit * 100))" ]; then                 (umask 077; set -C; printf '%s\n' deadline > "$__sl_control/deadline") || :                 rch_scope_launch_deadline_marker "$__sl_marker"                 rch_scope_launch_kill_self                 exit 255             fi             sleep 0.2 3>&- || exit 255         done     ) >/dev/null 2>&1 </dev/null &     # Watchdog must not inherit borrowed input. Post-admission failure is     # uncertain execution, never a pre-workload refusal permitting fallback.     rch_scope_launch_save_input || { rch_scope_launch_kill_self; exit 255; }     eval '"$@" 3>&- '"$__sl_input_redirect"' &'     __sl_payload=$!     rch_scope_launch_close_input     wait "$__sl_payload"; __sl_status=$?     [ "$__sl_cancelled" != 1 ] || __sl_status=143     # Payload status is NOT completion: the parent must prove tree cleanup.     (umask 077; set -C; printf 'RCH_SCOPED_RESULT_V1:%s:%s:%s:%s\n' \         "$__sl_build" "$rch_pgid" "$rch_start" "$__sl_status" > "$__sl_control/result.tmp") &&         mv "$__sl_control/result.tmp" "$__sl_control/result" || exit 255     while :; do sleep 1 3>&- || exit 255; done }  rch_scope_launch_read_result() {     [ -f "$__sl_control/result" ] && [ ! -L "$__sl_control/result" ] || return 1     # Balance case-pattern parentheses for Darwin's bash 3.2 substitution parser.     __sl_result_metadata=$(         case "$(uname -s)" in             (Darwin) stat -f '%u:%Lp:%l:%z' "$__sl_control/result";;             (Linux) stat -c '%u:%a:%h:%s' -- "$__sl_control/result";;             (*) exit 1;;         esac     ) || return 1     case "$__sl_result_metadata" in "$__sl_uid:600:1:"*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     __sl_result_size=${__sl_result_metadata##*:}     rch_scope_uint "$__sl_result_size" && [ "$__sl_result_size" -le 256 ] || return 1     __sl_result=$(cat "$__sl_control/result") || return 1     __sl_prefix="RCH_SCOPED_RESULT_V1:$__sl_build:$__sl_child:$__sl_child_start:"     case "$__sl_result" in "$__sl_prefix"*) ;; *) return 1;; esac     __sl_status=${__sl_result#"$__sl_prefix"}     rch_scope_uint "$__sl_status" && [ "${#__sl_status}" -le 3 ] && [ "$__sl_status" -le 255 ] || return 1     [ "$__sl_result_size" -eq "$((${#__sl_result} + 1))" ] &&         [ "$(wc -l < "$__sl_control/result")" -eq 1 ] }  rch_scope_launch_read_owned_record() {     rch_read_record "$__sl_record" "$__sl_build" &&         rch_scope_launch_binding_matches && [ "$rch_pgid" = "$__sl_child" ] &&         [ "$rch_start" = "$__sl_child_start" ] }  # Only before admission: stop the exact setup child. V2 also needs scope cleanup. rch_scope_launch_stop_setup() {     rch_scope_launch_process "$__sl_child"     case $? in 1) return 0;; 0) ;; *) return 1;; esac     [ "$rch_launch_birth" = "$__sl_child_start" ] || return 1     if [ "$rch_launch_group" = "$__sl_child" ]; then         kill -KILL -"$__sl_child" 2>/dev/null || return 1     else         kill -KILL "$__sl_child" 2>/dev/null || return 1     fi }  # Preserve child PID and FDs. Darwin supplies setsid(2) through Python. rch_scope_launch_exec() {     case "$(uname -s)" in         Linux) exec setsid "$@";;         Darwin) exec python3 -I -S -c 'import os, sys; os.setsid(); os.execvp(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[1:])' "$@";;         *) return 125;;     esac }  # Exit 255 retains ownership on unknown completion; it never permits fallback. rch_scope_launch_supervise() {     __sl_record=$1; __sl_limit=$2; __sl_marker=$3; __sl_build=$4; shift 4     case "$__sl_record" in /*) ;; *) return 125;; esac     # Atomic one-shot claim: neither lost publication nor retired receipt allows     # a second execution. Outer durable execution/source grants remain required.     if [ -e "$__sl_record" ] || [ -L "$__sl_record" ]; then         rch_scope_launch_message RCH_SCOPED_LAUNCH_ALREADY_OWNED >&2         return 255     fi     __sl_control="$__sl_record.supervisor.v1"     (umask 077; mkdir -m 700 -- "$__sl_control") 2>/dev/null || {         # Existing claims retain ownership; other mkdir errors started nothing.         if [ -e "$__sl_control" ] || [ -L "$__sl_control" ]; then             rch_scope_launch_message RCH_SCOPED_LAUNCH_ALREADY_OWNED >&2             return 255         fi         rch_scope_launch_message "$__sl_marker" setup >&2         return 125     }     # Recheck legacy writers; never remove or adopt their record or this fence.     if [ -e "$__sl_record" ] || [ -L "$__sl_record" ]; then         rch_scope_launch_message RCH_SCOPED_LAUNCH_ALREADY_OWNED >&2         return 255     fi     # Claim first: changed policy must not grant fallback to a duplicate launch.     rch_scope_launch_select || {         rch_scope_launch_message "$__sl_marker" setup >&2         return 125     }     rch_scope_uint "$__sl_limit" && [ "${#__sl_limit}" -le 10 ] &&         [ "$__sl_limit" -le 2147483647 ] || return 125     [ "$#" -gt 0 ] && rch_check_procfs_namespace || return 125     case "$(uname -s)" in         Linux) command -v setsid >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 125;;         Darwin) command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 125;;         *) return 125;;     esac     # Capture supervisor birth for the lost-parent watchdog before launch.     rch_scope_launch_process "$$" && rch_scope_launch_clock || {         rch_scope_launch_message "$__sl_marker" setup >&2         return 125     }     __sl_parent_start=$rch_launch_birth     __sl_uid=$(id -u) || return 125     __sl_cancelled=0     trap '__sl_cancelled=1' HUP INT TERM     rch_scope_launch_save_input || {         rch_scope_launch_message "$__sl_marker" setup >&2         return 125     }     # Only the fixed FD redirection becomes eval syntax; data stays quoted.     eval 'rch_scope_launch_exec /bin/sh -c "$rch_launch_source rch_scope_launch_child \"\$@\"" rch-scoped-child \         "$__sl_record" "$__sl_control" "$__sl_marker" "$__sl_build" \         "$$" "$__sl_parent_start" "$__sl_limit" \         "$rch_launch_nice" "$rch_launch_slice" "$rch_launch_manager" "$@" '"$__sl_input_redirect"' &'     __sl_child=$!     # Silence asynchronous shell notices after child stream inheritance. Route     # bounded diagnostics via stdout to original stderr; preserve closed stderr.     if rch_scope_launch_fd_open 2; then         rch_scope_launch_finish 1>&2 2>/dev/null     else         rch_scope_launch_finish >/dev/null 2>&1     fi }  # Must remain in the supervising shell for birth binding and cancellation traps. # stdout here is the original diagnostic stream, never payload stdout. rch_scope_launch_finish() {     rch_scope_launch_close_input     __sl_child_start=; __sl_admitted=0; __sl_admission_record=; __sl_reason=setup     __sl_admission_observations=0     rch_scope_launch_clock || return 255     __sl_setup_started=$rch_launch_now     __sl_last_clock=$rch_launch_now     while :; do         rch_scope_launch_process "$__sl_child"         __sl_live=$?         if [ "$__sl_live" = 0 ]; then             if [ -z "$__sl_child_start" ]; then __sl_child_start=$rch_launch_birth; fi             [ "$rch_launch_birth" = "$__sl_child_start" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         elif [ "$__sl_live" = 1 ]; then             __sl_reason=exited; break         else             __sl_reason=unknown; break         fi         if [ "$__sl_cancelled" = 1 ]; then __sl_reason=cancelled; break; fi         rch_scope_launch_clock || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         [ "$rch_launch_now" -ge "$__sl_last_clock" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         __sl_last_clock=$rch_launch_now         if [ "$__sl_admitted" = 0 ]; then             # One deadline covers publication and all admission observations.             if [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_setup_started))" -ge "$((6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))" ]; then                 [ "$__sl_admission_observations" = 0 ] || __sl_reason=unknown                 break             fi             if [ -e "$__sl_record" ] || [ -L "$__sl_record" ]; then                 if ! rch_scope_launch_read_owned_record; then                     __sl_reason=unknown; break                 fi                 # Live birth proves presence; only cleanup needs a full census.                 rch_remote_leader_matches || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 if [ $rch_scope_bound = 1 ]; then                     __sl_admission_observations=$((__sl_admission_observations + 1))                     rch_observation_deadline=$((__sl_setup_started + 6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))                     rch_observation_last_clock=$rch_launch_now                     rch_scope_operation state                     __sl_scope_state=$?                     unset rch_observation_deadline rch_observation_last_clock                     case "$__sl_scope_state" in                         1) ;;                         2)                             # Retry only unknown with gate closed. Each pass                             # revalidates child birth and the complete receipt.                             if [ "$__sl_admission_observations" -lt 3 ]; then                                 sleep 0.1 || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                                 continue                             fi                             __sl_reason=unknown; break;;                         *) __sl_reason=unknown; break;;                     esac                 fi                 rch_scope_launch_clock || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 [ "$rch_launch_now" -ge "$__sl_last_clock" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 __sl_last_clock=$rch_launch_now                 if [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_setup_started))" -ge "$((6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))" ]; then                     __sl_reason=unknown; break                 fi                 if [ "$__sl_cancelled" = 1 ]; then __sl_reason=cancelled; break; fi                 rch_remote_leader_matches || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 __sl_started=$rch_launch_now                 # Mark admitted BEFORE gate write. Retain its binding in memory:                 # receipt loss/replacement cannot redirect or waive cleanup.                 __sl_admission_record=$rch_record                 __sl_admitted=1                 (umask 077; set -C; printf '%s\n' go > "$__sl_control/go") || {                     __sl_reason=unknown; break;                 }             fi         else             if [ -e "$__sl_control/result" ] || [ -L "$__sl_control/result" ]; then                 if rch_scope_launch_read_result; then __sl_reason=result; else __sl_reason=unknown; fi                 break             fi             if [ "$__sl_limit" -gt 0 ] && [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_started))" -ge "$((__sl_limit * 100))" ]; then                 __sl_reason=deadline; break             fi         fi         sleep 0.1 || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }     done     __sl_cleanup=0     __sl_cleanup_status=0; __sl_record_status=0     if [ "$__sl_admitted" = 1 ]; then         # Revalidate signal authority and prove emptiness using admitted identity.         rch_remote_cancel_loaded kill || {             __sl_cleanup_status=$?; __sl_cleanup=1;         }         # Check record AFTER cleanup in a subshell; parsing cannot replace our         # admitted identity. Record damage still forbids confirmed completion.         (rch_scope_launch_read_owned_record &&             [ "$rch_record" = "$__sl_admission_record" ]) || {                 __sl_record_status=$?; __sl_cleanup=1;             }     else         rch_scope_launch_stop_setup || {             __sl_cleanup_status=$?; __sl_cleanup=1;         }         # Reap before reading: setup publication cannot then race recovery.         if [ "$__sl_cleanup" = 0 ]; then wait "$__sl_child"; __sl_wait=$?; fi         if [ -e "$__sl_record" ] || [ -L "$__sl_record" ]; then             if rch_scope_launch_read_owned_record; then                 rch_remote_cancel_loaded kill || {                     __sl_cleanup_status=$?; __sl_cleanup=1;                 }             else                 __sl_record_status=$?; __sl_cleanup=1             fi         fi     fi     if [ "$__sl_cleanup" != 0 ]; then         rch_scope_launch_message "RCH_SCOPED_COMPLETION_UNCONFIRMED RCH_SCOPED_COMPLETION_DETAIL:reason=$__sl_reason,admitted=$__sl_admitted,cleanup=$__sl_cleanup_status,record=$__sl_record_status"         return 255     fi     if [ "$__sl_admitted" = 1 ]; then wait "$__sl_child"; __sl_wait=$?; fi     # Deadline wins raced payload status.
