        if [ "$__sl_live" = 0 ]; then             if [ -z "$__sl_child_start" ]; then __sl_child_start=$rch_launch_birth; fi             [ "$rch_launch_birth" = "$__sl_child_start" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         elif [ "$__sl_live" = 1 ]; then             __sl_reason=exited; break         else             __sl_reason=unknown; break         fi         if [ "$__sl_cancelled" = 1 ]; then __sl_reason=cancelled; break; fi         rch_scope_launch_clock || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         [ "$rch_launch_now" -ge "$__sl_last_clock" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }         __sl_last_clock=$rch_launch_now         if [ "$__sl_admitted" = 0 ]; then             # One deadline covers publication and all admission observations.             if [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_setup_started))" -ge "$((6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))" ]; then                 [ "$__sl_admission_observations" = 0 ] || __sl_reason=unknown                 break             fi             if [ -e "$__sl_record" ] || [ -L "$__sl_record" ]; then                 if ! rch_scope_launch_read_owned_record; then                     __sl_reason=unknown; break                 fi                 # Live birth proves presence; only cleanup needs a full census.                 rch_remote_leader_matches || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 if [ $rch_scope_bound = 1 ]; then                     __sl_admission_observations=$((__sl_admission_observations + 1))                     rch_observation_deadline=$((__sl_setup_started + 6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))                     rch_observation_last_clock=$rch_launch_now                     rch_scope_operation state                     __sl_scope_state=$?                     unset rch_observation_deadline rch_observation_last_clock                     case "$__sl_scope_state" in                         1) ;;                         2)                             # Retry only unknown with gate closed. Each pass                             # revalidates child birth and the complete receipt.                             if [ "$__sl_admission_observations" -lt 3 ]; then                                 sleep 0.1 || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                                 continue                             fi                             __sl_reason=unknown; break;;                         *) __sl_reason=unknown; break;;                     esac                 fi                 rch_scope_launch_clock || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 [ "$rch_launch_now" -ge "$__sl_last_clock" ] || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 __sl_last_clock=$rch_launch_now                 if [ "$((rch_launch_now - __sl_setup_started))" -ge "$((6000 * rch_timing_pct / 100))" ]; then                     __sl_reason=unknown; break                 fi                 if [ "$__sl_cancelled" = 1 ]; then __sl_reason=cancelled; break; fi                 rch_remote_leader_matches || { __sl_reason=unknown; break; }                 __sl_started=$rch_launch_now                 # Mark admitted BEFORE gate write. 775238 python3 -I -S -c """Worker-local build disk admission and early termination (Python stdlib only).  Embedded by RCH so the SSH execution path cannot depend on an upgraded worker binary. Polling is not a filesystem quota: fast bursts can overrun an interval, and process groups do not contain a child deliberately starting another session. Use the existing cgroup/service scope for that stronger process containment. No output is removed here. Reservations are retired only after their group is observed dead, never by TTL, PID reuse, or an incomplete directory scan. """  import concurrent.futures import contextlib import errno import fcntl import hashlib import json import os import signal import stat import subprocess import sys import tempfile import time import uuid   def timing_scale():     """RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT (whole percent, set by the RCH client from RCH_TIMING_SCALE): 100..1000, bad value -> 1.0."""     raw = os.environ.get("RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT", "")     return int(raw) / 100.0 if raw.isascii() and raw.isdigit() and raw[0] != "0" and 100 <= int(raw) <= 1000 else 1.0   # Local patch 6: one multiplier for every fixed DEADLINE below (intervals and poll rates are not scaled). SCALE = timing_scale() POLL_SECONDS = 0.25 # Local patch 4 (fleksprime owner, 5 Nov 2026): was 1.0. Under CPU starvation (load ~40 on 18 cores) one statvfs # sample in this Python monitor took >1 s and RCH killed a healthy build with E330 'free-space telemetry deadline'. # Samples are still requested every POLL_SECONDS; only the declare-the-monitor-dead deadline is longer. FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE = 5.0 * SCALE # Local patch 3 (fleksprime owner, 5 Nov 2026): was 2.0. Each full walk of a large cargo target cost 1-2 s of CPU per # build; the per-build output check now runs every 10 s. Free-space sampling (POLL_SECONDS) is unchanged. SCAN_INTERVAL = 10.0 SCAN_DEADLINE = 30.0 * SCALE HEARTBEAT_DEADLINE = 3.0 * SCALE LOCK_DEADLINE = 2.0 * SCALE ADMIT_DEADLINE = 5.0 * SCALE EXIT_BUDGET = 120 MAX_LEDGER_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024 MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES = 8192 # Extra learning metadata is bounded independently of the existing output # scan. Oversized or changing trees still receive the ordinary disk guards; # they simply cannot attest a smaller, pool-dependent reservation. MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES = 100_000 # A worker's shared Cargo cache can hold 600,000 entries, and one cold walk took # 30 seconds on an idle worker. Every build walks its outputs each SCAN_INTERVAL # under SCAN_DEADLINE, so walking a cache that size stops builds on a busy worker # and exhausts the entries above. Past this bound the cache keeps its reservation # and floor protection, but its existing and new bytes are not charged to a build. MAX_WALKED_CACHE_ENTRIES = 25_000 DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX = "_DISK_BUDGET:"   class BudgetFailure(Exception):     def __init__(self, reason, free=0, growth=0, detail="", required_growth_bytes=None): positive_int(value):     return type(value) is int and 0 < value <= (1 << 63) - 1   def nonnegative_int(value):     return type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= (1 << 63) - 1   def growth_buffer(budget):     return min(256 * 1024 * 1024, max(1, budget // 16))   def validate_warm_policy(policy, budget):     if policy is None:         return      def digest(value):         return (isinstance(value, list) and len(value) == 32                 and all(type(byte) is int and 0 <= byte <= 255 for byte in value)                 and any(value))      def pool(value):         return (isinstance(value, dict)                 and set(value) == {"identity", "state", "retained_bytes"}                 and digest(value["identity"]) and digest(value["state"])                 and positive_int(value["retained_bytes"]))      if (not positive_int(budget)             or not isinstance(policy, dict)             or set(policy) != {"cold_growth_bytes", "pools"}             or not positive_int(policy["cold_growth_bytes"])             or policy["cold_growth_bytes"] <= budget             or not isinstance(policy["pools"], list) or len(policy["pools"]) != 8             or not BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="duplicate warm disk pool identity")   def boot_identity():     if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):         with open("/proc/self/status", encoding="ascii") as stream:             namespace = [line.split()[1:] for line in stream if line.startswith("NSpid:")]         # A foreign procfs cannot prove that a recorded process group is gone.         if namespace != [[str(os.getpid())]]:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unverified process namespace")         with open("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id", raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid kernel boot identity")     return boot   def linux_process(path):     with open(path, encoding="utf-8", errors="strict") as stream:     }   def process_snapshot(heartbeat=lambda: None):     result = {}     if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):         with os.scandir("/proc") as entries:             for entry in entries:                 # A large, progressing census is not a wedged kernel read.                 # Keep the guardian armed between records; any individual                 # scandir/read stall still receives no heartbeat and is fenced.                 heartbeat()                 if not entry.name.isdecimal():                     continue                 try:                     record = linux_process(entry.path + "/stat")                     result[record["pid"]] = record                     # A dead main thread does not prove that its sibling                     # threads stopped writing. Linux retains their group.                     if record["state"] in ("Z", "X"):                         with os.scandir(entry.path + "/task") as tasks:                             for task in tasks:                                 heartbeat()                                 try:                                     thread = linux_process(task.path + "/stat")                                     result[thread["pid"]] = thread                                 except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                                     pass                 except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                     # An opened procfs record can return ESRCH when its task                     # exits before read, rather than ENOENT from open.                     pass     elif sys.platform == "darwin":         census = subprocess.Popen(             ["ps", "-axo", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,stat="], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, text=True         )         try:             output, _ = census.communicate(timeout=1)         except BaseException:             census.kill()             census.communicate()             raise         if census.returncode:             raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(census.returncode, census.args, output)         for line in output.splitlines():             heartbeat()             pid, parent, group, state = line.split()             # This exact child has been reaped above. Its census entry is not             # an outstanding build writer, even though ps observed itself.             if int(pid) == census.pid:                 continue             result[int(pid)] = {                 "pid": int(pid), "parent": int(parent), "group": int(group),                 "state": state[0], "start": None,             }     else:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="process census is unavailable")     if os.getpid() not in result:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="process census omitted supervisor")     return result   def darwin_leader_start(group, boot):     # Native witness rejects dead/zombie leaders and rechecks birth and group     # membership. A whole-machine ps census adds no owner identity evidence.     witness = subprocess.check_output(         ["rch-wkr", "process-identity", str(group)], timeout=1, text=True     ).split()     if len(witness) != 3 or witness[0] != boot or int(witness[2]) != group:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unverified Darwin group identity")     return witness[1]   def leader_start(group, snapshot):     record = snapshot.get(group)     if record is None or record["state"] in ("Z", "X"):         raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader disappeared")     if sys.platform == "darwin":         # ps display timestamps are too coarse to fence PID reuse. Live group membership wins.     return record["boot"] == boot and record["group"] in groups   def filesystem_sample(paths):     samples = {}     for path in paths:         # Bind capacity to the same directory as its device identity. Separate         # path-based stat/statvfs calls can combine different filesystems when         # a symlink or mount path changes between the two operations.         descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_CLOEXEC)         try:             device = os.fstat(descriptor).st_dev             info = os.fstatvfs(descriptor)         finally:             os.close(descriptor)         available = info.f_bavail * info.f_frsize         samples[device] = min(samples.get(device, available), available)     return samples   def output_bindings(paths):     identities = {}     for path in paths:         info = os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False)         if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output root is not a physical directory")         identities[path] = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)     return identities   def verify_output_bindings(identities):     if output_bindings(identities) != identities:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output root changed during execution")   def validate_cargo_cache_plan(plan, required=False):     if type(required) is not bool:         raise            "monitor_unavailable",             detail="Cargo cache scope is unresolved; use a literal absolute CARGO_HOME and supported env prefix",         )   def prepare_cargo_cache_roots(plan):     """Bind the worker's isolated home and only Cargo's known cache redirects.      The transfer shell resolves RCH_CH_BASE before client env forwarding and     before cd. Never expand a client path or walk arbitrary cache symlinks.     At most six individual directories are created before admission; missing     ancestor trees are refused rather than seeded outside the reservation.     """     if plan is None:         return [], {}     validate_cargo_cache_plan(plan)     base = os.environ.get("RCH_CH_BASE", "")     if (not os.path.isabs(base) or os.path.realpath(base) != base             or not os.path.isdir(base)):         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker Cargo cache base is not a resolved physical directory")     homes = [os.path.join(base, plan["name"])]     if plan["additional_home"] is not None:         homes.append(plan["additional_home"])     aliases = {}     for home in dict.fromkeys(homes):         for logical in (home, os.path.join(home, "registry"), os.path.join(home, "git")):             physical = os.path.realpath(logical)             if not os.path.isdir(os.path.dirname(physical)):                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache parent must already exist before admission")             try:                 os.mkdir(physical)             except FileExistsError:                 pass             if not stat.S_ISDIR(os.stat(physical, follow_symlinks=False).st_mode):                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache output root is not a physical directory")             aliases[logical] = physical     verify_cargo_cache_bindings(aliases)     return list(dict.fromkeys(aliases.values())), aliases   def cache_walk_is_bounded(roots):     """Stop counting at the bound, so a large cache costs one small walk per build."""     entries, visited, pending = 0, set(), list(roots)     while pending:         path = pending.pop()         # Roots nest, and each directory's entries count only once.         if path in visited:             continue         visited.add(path)         with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError, NotADirectoryError), os.scandir(path) as listing:             for entry in listing:                 entries += 1                 if entries > MAX_WALKED_CACHE_ENTRIES:                     return False                 if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):                     pending.append(entry.path)     return True   def verify_cargo_cache_bindings(aliases):     for logical, physical in aliases.items():         if os.path.realpath(logical) != physical:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache redirect changed during execution")   class OutputScan:     def __init__(self, total, records=None, pool_state=None, pool_bytes=0, added=None):         self.total, self.records = total, records         self.pool_state, self.pool_bytes = pool_state, pool_bytes         self.added = added   def output_signature(info):     # A modified/reused inode cannot retain its old allocation credit. Merely     # subtracting its starting size would miss replacement output after cleanup.     # Atime is deliberately absent: reading a retained artifact is not a write.     return (info.st_blocks * 512, info.st_mode, info.st_size,             info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns)   def measure_outputs(paths, target=None, baseline_records=None):     """Allocated bytes and bounded inode evidence; never follow symlinks.      Scan the target first so overlapping source/output roots cannot remove     entries from its fingerprint through cross-root inode deduplication. Pool     names and signatures are sorted only after collecting a bounded snapshot.     Runtime additions stream against the immutable baseline even after the     optional current snapshot exceeds its bound or loses fingerprint evidence.     """     total, pool_bytes, seen = 0, 0, set()     added = 0 if baseline_records is not None else None     records, pool_entries = {}, []     devices = {identity[0] for identity in output_bindings(paths).values()}      def incomplete():         nonlocal records, pool_entries         records, pool_entries = None, None      def visit(name, parent=None, relative=None):         nonlocal total, pool_bytes, added         try:             info = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)             identity = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)             signature = output_signature(info)             if relative is not None and pool_entries is not None:                 if len(pool_entries) >= MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES:                     incomplete()                 else:                     pool_entries.append((relative, *identity, *signature))             if identity in seen:                 return             if info.st_dev not in devices:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unmonitored filesystem inside output root")             seen.add(identity)             total += signature[0]             if added is not None and baseline_records.get(identity) != signature:                 added += signature[0]             if relative is not None:                 pool_bytes += signature[0]             if records is not None:                 if len(records) >= MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES:                     incomplete()                 else:                     records[identity] = signature             if stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):                 descriptor = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)                 try:                     current = os.fstat(descriptor)                     if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != identity:                         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output directory changed during scan")                     if output_signature(current) != signature:                         incomplete()                     with os.scandir(descriptor) as entries:                         for entry in entries:                             child = None if relative is None else relative + "/" + entry.name                             visit(entry.name, descriptor, child)                     # A tree that changed while enumerated cannot certify an                     # exact retained-pool proof, even if its byte total fits.                     if output_signature(os.fstat(descriptor)) != signature:                         incomplete()                 finally:                     os.close(descriptor)         except OSError as error:             # Cargo renames and removes intermediates while compiling. A             # disappearing managed root is separately rejected by its binding.             if error.errno not in (errno.ENOENT, errno.ENOTDIR, errno.ELOOP):                 raise             incomplete()     try:         ordered = paths if target is None else list(dict.fromkeys([target] + paths))         for path in ordered:             visit(path, relative="." if path == target else None)         pool_state = None         if target is not None and pool_entries is not None:             fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(b"rch-pool-state-v1\0")             for entry in sorted(pool_entries):                 fingerprint.update(json.dumps(entry, separators=(",", ":")).encode("ascii"))                 fingerprint.update(b"\n")             pool_state = list(fingerprint.digest())         return OutputScan(total, records, pool_state, pool_bytes, added)     finally:         # Recursion captures this function's own cell. Break that cycle on         # success AND failure so every scan releases its inode table promptly,         # instead of accumulating entire trees until cyclic GC runs.         visit = None   def private_regular(descriptor):     info = os.fstat(descriptor)     if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unsafe worker accounting file")   def atomic_json(path, value):     descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".disk-", dir=os.path.dirname(path)) path)   class Registry:     def __init__(self, root=None):         self.root = root or "/var/tmp/rch-build-disk-" + str(os.getuid())         try:             os.mkdir(self.root, 0o700)         except raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unsafe worker accounting directory")      @contextlib.contextmanager     def locked(self):         descriptor = os.open(self.root + "/lock", os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)         try:             private_regular(descriptor)             deadline = time.monotonic() + LOCK_DEADLINE             while True:                 try:                  raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker reservation lock deadline")                     time.sleep(0.025)             yield         finally:             os.close(descriptor)      def read(self):         try:             descriptor = os.open(self.root + "/ledger.json", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)         except FileNotFoundError:             return [] disk reservation")         return entries      def admit(self, monitor):         # Counting every reservation is conservative and avoids a whole-host         # process census on the common path. Retire records only when capacity         # or the ledger limit requires it, using the authoritative census.         with self.locked():             records = self.read()             free = monitor.sample()             if set(free) != monitor.devices:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="filesystem changed before admission")              def fits():                 return all(available >= max([monitor.floor] + [entry["floor"] for entry in records if device in entry["devices"]])                            + monitor.budget + sum(entry["budget"] for entry in records if device in entry["devices"])                            for device, available in free.items())              if len(records) >= 4096 or not fits():                 groups = alive_groups(process_snapshot())                 records = [entry for entry in records if reservation_alive(entry, monitor.boot, groups)]                 free = monitor.sample()                 if set(free) != monitor.devices:                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="filesystem changed before admission")             if len(records) >= 4096:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker disk ledger reservation limit")             floors = {}             for device, available in free.items():                 same_device = [entry for entry in records if device in entry["devices"]]                 floor = max([monitor.floor] + [entry["floor"] for entry in same_device])                 required = floor + monitor.budget + sum(entry["budget"] for entry in same_device)                 if available < required:                     raise BudgetFailure("admission", available, detail="free space cannot cover active build reservations and protected floor")                 floors[device] = floor             records.append({                 "boot": monitor.boot, "group": monitor.group, "leader_start": monitor.start,                 "devices": sorted(monitor.devices), "budget": monitor.budget, "floor": monitor.floor,             })             atomic_json(self.root + "/ledger.json", {"version": 1, "entries": records})             return free, floors   class Monitor:     def __init__(self, budget, floor, target, paths, outputs=None, cargo_cache=None):         self.budget, self.floor = budget, floor         self.target = target         self.boot, self.group = boot_identity(), os.getpgrp()         cache_outputs, self.cargo_cache_bindings = prepare_cargo_cache_roots(cargo_cache)         walked_cache = cache_outputs if cache_walk_is_bounded(cache_outputs) else []         self.outputs = list(dict.fromkeys([target] + (outputs or []) + walked_cache))         # Every managed output volume needs both a reservation and ongoing         # floor protection, even when a caller omitted it from its probe list.         self.paths = list(dict.fromkeys(paths + [target] + self.outputs + cache_outputs))         self.bindings = output_bindings(self.outputs)         if self.group <= 1:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="build has no isolated process group")         snapshot = process_snapshot()         self.start = leader_start(self.group, snapshot)         # Keep the command's enclosing shells alive on successful completion.         self.controls = set()         parent = os.getpid()         while parent in snapshot and snapshot[parent]["group"] == self.group:             self.controls.add(parent)             parent = snapshot[parent]["parent"]         self.parent = os.getppid()         self.devices = set(self.sample())         identity = ["rch-pool-identity-v1", os.path.abspath(target), self.boot,                     *self.bindings[target]]         self.pool_identity = list(hashlib.sha256(             json.dumps(identity, separators=(",", ":")).encode("ascii")         ).digest())         initial = self.scan()         self.baseline, self.baseline_records = initial.total, initial.records         self.baseline_pool = self.pool_observation(initial)         self.final_pool, self.admitted_pool = self.baseline_pool, None         self.incremental_reliable = initial.records is not None and self.baseline_pool is not None         self.peak_added, self.unproven_baseline, self.strict_tracking = 0, 0, False         self.peak_output, self.peak_growth = self.baseline, 0         self.free = {}         self.free_start, self.free_min = {}, {}         self.floors = {device: floor for device in self.devices}         self.previous = None      def sample(self):         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         samples = filesystem_sample(self.paths)         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         return samples      def scan(self):         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         result = measure_outputs(self.outputs, self.target, getattr(self, "baseline_records", None))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         return result      def pool_observation(self, scan):         if scan.pool_state is None:             return None         return {"identity": self.pool_identity, "state": scan.pool_state,                 "retained_bytes": scan.pool_bytes}      def require_runtime_tracking(self, required):         if type(required) is not bool:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid required output tracking mode")         if required and self.baseline_records is None:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="bounded initial output tracking is unavailable")         self.strict_tracking = self.strict_tracking or required      def require_warm_pool(self, policy):         validate_warm_policy(policy, self.budget)         if policy is None:             return         if self.baseline_pool is None or self.baseline_pool not in policy["pools"]:             raise BudgetFailure(                 "cold_required", detail="retained output pool no longer matches its warm reservation",                 required_growth_bytes=policy["cold_growth_bytes"],             )         # Only the attested target pool has already been funded. Source sync,         # caches, and other roots cannot borrow its retained-artifact credit.         # Keep a growth buffer available before installing the reservation;         # a changed source baseline must request cold admission, not silently         # consume the whole allowance that was selected for this build.         unproven = self.baseline - self.baseline_pool["retained_bytes"]         if unproven + growth_buffer(self.budget) >= self.budget:             raise BudgetFailure(                 "cold_required",                 detail=f"unproven output baseline ({unproven} bytes) leaves insufficient warm growth allowance",                 required_growth_bytes=policy["cold_growth_bytes"],             )         self.unproven_baseline = unproven         self.strict_tracking = True         self.peak_growth = max(self.peak_growth, unproven)         # Admission proof survives a later loss of optional learning metadata.         # Losing that metadata must not turn confirmed completion into replay.         self.admitted_pool = self.baseline_pool      def admit(self):         self.free, self.floors = Registry().admit(self)         self.free_start, self.free_min = dict(self.free), dict(self.free)         self.previous = (time.monotonic(), self.free)      def observe_free(self, samples, now):         if set(samples) != self.devices:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="monitored filesystem identity changed")         for device, free in samples.items():             self.free_min[device] = min(self.free_min.get(device, free), free)             base = growth_buffer(self.budget)             margin = base             if self.previous is not None:                 previous_time, previous_samples = self.previous                 rate = max(0, previous_samples[device] - free) / max(now - previous_time, 0.001)                 margin = max(base, int(rate * 2))             if free <= self.floors[device] + margin:                 raise BudgetFailure("free_floor", free, self.peak_growth, "protected floor or observed write-rate buffer reached")         self.free, self.previous = samples, (now, samples)      def observe_output(self, scan):         self.peak_output = max(self.peak_output, scan.total)         net_growth = max(0, scan.total - self.baseline)         self.final_pool = self.pool_observation(scan)         if scan.records is None or self.final_pool is None:             self.incremental_reliable = False         added = scan.added         if added is None and self.baseline_records is not None and scan.records is not None:             added = sum(signature[0] for identity, signature in scan.records.items()                         if self.baseline_records.get(identity) != signature)         if added is None and self.strict_tracking:             # Never turn an already-small reservation into net-only tracking.             # Unknown attribution receives no retained credit. Confirmed             # completion remains possible if this conservative charge fits.             self.incremental_reliable = False             added = scan.total         if added is not None:             self.peak_added = max(self.peak_added, added)             # Old-artifact cleanup cannot erase the new output's reservation             # charge. Other jobs' overlapping output may be charged too, never             # divided into unsupported per-writer attribution.         # Preserve the unproven baseline charge even if a later scan loses         # bounded evidence. Changed unproven inodes may be charged once at         # admission and again as new output; that conservative aggregate can         # exceed the largest simultaneously allocated output snapshot.         self.peak_growth = max(             self.peak_growth, self.unproven_baseline + max(net_growth, self.peak_added)         )         if self.peak_growth > self.budget:             raise BudgetFailure("growth", min(self.free.values(), default=0), self.peak_growth, "allocated build output exceeded its reservation")      def check_owner(self):         if os.getppid() != self.parent:             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="disk guardian disappeared")         if sys.platform == "darwin":             if darwin_leader_start(self.group, self.boot) != self.start:                 raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader changed")             return         if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):             try:                 snapshot = {self.group: linux_process("/proc/" + str(self.group) + "/stat")}             except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                 snapshot = {}         else:             snapshot = process_snapshot()         if leader_start(self.group, snapshot) != self.start:             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader changed")   def kill_owned_group(group):     # Our own live membership prevents PGID reuse, even after the leader died     # or process telemetry failed. Never signal an arbitrary ledger PID/group.     if group <= 1 or os.getpgrp() != group:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="lost own build process group")     os.killpg(group, signal.SIGKILL)   def receipts_for_failure(marker, failure, budget, floor, started, monitor):     receipt = {         "reason": failure.reason, "free_bytes": failure.free, "growth_bytes": failure.growth,         "max_growth_bytes": budget, "min_free_bytes": floor, "started": started,         "detail": failure.detail[:240],         "peak_output_bytes": monitor.peak_output if monitor is not None else None,     }     if failure.reason == "cold_required":         receipt["required_growth_bytes"] = failure.required_growth_bytes     receipts = [(marker, receipt)]     if started and monitor is not None:         receipts.append(usage_receipt(marker, monitor, False))     return receipts   def usage_receipt(marker, monitor, complete, exit_code=None):     incremental = None     if monitor.incremental_reliable:         incremental = {             "version": 1, "baseline_output_bytes": monitor.baseline,             "peak_added_output_bytes": monitor.peak_added,             "peak_free_drop_bytes": sum(max(0, start - monitor.free_min[device])                                         for device, start in monitor.free_start.items()),             "baseline_pool": monitor.baseline_pool, "final_pool": monitor.final_pool,         }     return (marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)] + "_DISK_USAGE:", {         "max_growth_bytes": monitor.budget, "min_free_bytes": monitor.floor,         "peak_output_bytes": monitor.peak_output, "peak_growth_bytes": monitor.peak_growth,         "incremental": incremental, "admitted_pool": monitor.admitted_pool,         "complete": complete, "exit_code": exit_code,     })   def receipt_text(receipts):     return "".join(marker + (json.dumps(value, separators=(",", ":")) if value is not None else "") + "\n" for marker, value in receipts)   def persist_receipts(path, receipts):     descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".disk-receipt-", dir=os.path.dirname(path))     with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:         stream.write(receipt_text(receipts))         stream.flush()         os.fsync(stream.fileno())     os.replace(temporary, path)   def emit_receipts(receipts):     # This is a supplement to the durable receipt. Never wait for a noisy     # workload's full stderr before stopping its writers; restore fd flags.     flags = fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_GETFL)     try:         fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_SETFL, flags | os.O_NONBLOCK)         with contextlib.suppress(OSError):             os.write(2, ("\n" + receipt_text(receipts)).encode())     finally:         with contextlib.suppress(OSError):             fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_SETFL, flags)   def bounded_result(future, deadline, heartbeat):     while not future.done():         heartbeat()         if time.monotonic() >= deadline:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="disk probe or scan deadline reached")         time.sleep(0.025)     return future.result()   def finish_descendants(monitor, heartbeat):     deadline = None     while True:         heartbeat()         monitor.check_owner()         snapshot = process_snapshot(heartbeat)         descendants = [record for record in snapshot.values()                        if record["group"] == monitor.group and record["pid"] not in monitor.controls                        and record["state"] not in ("Z", "X")]         if not descendants:             return         # A progressing first census can be slow on a busy worker. Give the         # writers their bounded exit window after discovering them, rather         # than expiring it before the first signal. The guardian still fences         # a stalled census, and completion still requires an empty census.         if deadline is None:             deadline = time.monotonic() + 2         for record in descendants:             # Pin the process on Linux so a recycled numeric PID cannot receive             # a signal between membership inspection and signal delivery.             with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError):                 if hasattr(os, "pidfd_open") and hasattr(signal, "pidfd_send_signal"):                     descriptor = os.pidfd_open(record["pid"])                     try:                         if os.getpgid(record["pid"]) == monitor.group:                             signal.pidfd_send_signal(descriptor, signal.SIGKILL)                     finally:                         os.close(descriptor)                 elif os.getpgid(record["pid"]) == monitor.group:                     # Native Unix cancellation already uses an owned group.                     # Without pidfd, stop that still-owned group rather than                     # risk signalling a recycled individual PID.                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="background writer cleanup requires whole-group cancellation")         if time.monotonic() >= deadline:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="background build writers did not stop")         time.sleep(0.025)   def supervise(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, heartbeat, timeout_seconds=0, receipt_initialized=False, warm_policy=None, require_incremental_tracking=False, cargo_cache=None, cargo_cache_required=False):     monitor = child = None     started = receipt_owned = False     executor = concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2)     try:         if receipt_path is not None:             if receipt_initialized:                 descriptor = os.open(receipt_path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)                 with os.fdopen(descriptor, "r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:                     private_regular(stream.fileno())                     if stream.read(MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES + 1) != marker + "\n":                         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="disk receipt initialization does not match this attempt")             else:                 descriptor = os.open(receipt_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)                 os.close(descriptor)             receipt_owned = True         validate_warm_policy(warm_policy, budget)         validate_cargo_cache_plan(cargo_cache, cargo_cache_required)         monitor = bounded_result(             executor.submit(Monitor, budget, floor, target, paths, outputs, cargo_cache),             time.monotonic() + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat,         )         monitor.require_warm_pool(warm_policy)         monitor.require_runtime_tracking(require_incremental_tracking)         bounded_result(executor.submit(monitor.admit), time.monotonic() + ADMIT_DEADLINE, heartbeat)          def interrupted(signum, frame):             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="supervisor received signal " + str(signum))          for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):             signal.signal(signum, interrupted)         # Signal delivery can interrupt Popen after the OS created its child,         # before Python returns it. That is possibly started, never a retryable         # admission refusal. A group kill does not require a returned child PID.         started = True         heartbeat(b"S")         execution_started = time.monotonic()         child = subprocess.Popen(command, close_fds=True)         free_future = executor.submit(monitor.sample)         free_started = time.monotonic()         scan_future = executor.submit(monitor.scan)         scan_started = free_started         next_scan = scan_started + SCAN_INTERVAL         while child.poll() is None:             heartbeat()             monitor.check_owner()             now = time.monotonic()             if timeout_seconds and now - execution_started >= timeout_seconds:                 raise BudgetFailure("deadline", detail="remote execution deadline reached")             if free_future.done():                 monitor.observe_free(free_future.result(), now)                 free_future = executor.submit(monitor.sample)                 free_started = now             elif now - free_started > FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="free-space telemetry deadline")             if scan_future is not None:                 if scan_future.done():                     monitor.observe_output(scan_future.result())                     scan_future = None                 elif now - scan_started > SCAN_DEADLINE:                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output scan deadline")             if scan_future is None and now >= next_scan:                 scan_future = executor.submit(monitor.scan)                 scan_started, next_scan = now, now + SCAN_INTERVAL             time.sleep(POLL_SECONDS)         finish_descendants(monitor, heartbeat)         monitor.observe_free(bounded_result(free_future, free_started + FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE, heartbeat), time.monotonic())         if scan_future is not None:             monitor.observe_output(bounded_result(scan_future, scan_started + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat))         monitor.observe_output(bounded_result(executor.submit(monitor.scan), time.monotonic() + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat))         # The in-flight free-space sample can predate the command's last writes         # (or the entire command). A final output scan can also take seconds         # while unrelated jobs consume this filesystem. Only a NEW probe after         # writers stop and scanning finishes can attest the completion floor.         monitor.observe_free(bounded_result(             executor.submit(monitor.sample), time.monotonic() + FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE, heartbeat,         ), time.monotonic())         # Completion means the foreground command ended, remaining writers         # stopped, and final output and free-space checks passed. A nonzero exit         # remains a complete measurement; callers gate successful learning on         # that independent command status.         exit_code = child.returncode if child.returncode >= 0 else 128 - child.returncode         receipts = [usage_receipt(marker, monitor, True, exit_code)]         if receipt_owned:             persist_receipts(receipt_path, receipts)         emit_receipts(receipts)         return exit_code     except BaseException as error:         try:             failure = error if isinstance(error, BudgetFailure) else BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail=str(error))             if failure.reason == "deadline":                 receipts = [(marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)], None)]                 if monitor is not None:                     receipts.append(usage_receipt(marker, monitor, False))             else:                 receipts = receipts_for_failure(marker, failure, budget, floor, started, monitor)             # A failing filesystem can also wedge receipt fsync. Publish the             # bounded, nonblocking failure diagnostic first so the guardian's             # subsequent kill does not erase the reason. This is never a             # completion acknowledgement; durable success still comes first.             with contextlib.suppress(OSError):                 emit_receipts(receipts)             if receipt_owned:                 with contextlib.suppress(OSError):                     persist_receipts(receipt_path, receipts)         finally:             # A broken serializer, receipt writer, or diagnostic must never             # bypass the only cleanup boundary for a possibly started child.             if started:                 kill_owned_group(os.getpgrp())         return EXIT_BUDGET     finally:         # os._exit below terminates outstanding sampler threads too. Avoid         # cancel_futures (Python 3.9+) on workers with Python 3.8.         executor.shutdown(wait=False)   def run_guarded(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, mode, timeout_seconds=0, receipt_initialized=False, warm_policy=None, require_incremental_tracking=False, cargo_cache=None, cargo_cache_required=False):     if mode == "standalone":         os.setsid()     elif mode != "tracked":         raise ValueError("invalid build supervisor mode")     group, parent = os.getpgrp(), os.getppid()     read_fd, write_fd = os.pipe()     os.set_blocking(read_fd, False)     os.set_blocking(write_fd, False)     supervisor = os.fork()     if supervisor == 0:         os.close(read_fd)          def heartbeat(value=b"H"):             if os.getppid() != parent_guardian:                 raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="disk guardian disappeared")             with contextlib.suppress(BlockingIOError):                 os.write(write_fd, value)          parent_guardian = os.getppid()         result = supervise(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, heartbeat, timeout_seconds, receipt_initialized, warm_policy, require_incremental_tracking, cargo_cache, cargo_cache_required)         os.write(write_fd, b"C")         os._exit(result)     os.close(write_fd)     cancelled, started, completed = False, False, False      def interrupted(signum, frame):         nonlocal cancelled         cancelled = True      def stop(reason, **detail):         # Diagnostics must not delay the existing kill fence. This separate         # marker never attests disk usage or successful command completion.         try:             emit_receipts([(marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)] + "_DISK_GUARDIAN:", {                 "reason": reason, "started": started, **detail,             })])         finally:             kill_owned_group(group)      for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):         signal.signal(signum, interrupted)     last_heartbeat = time.monotonic()     while True:         try:             data = os.read(read_fd, 4096)             if data:                 started = started or b"S" in data                 completed = completed or b"C" in data                 last_heartbeat = time.monotonic()         except BlockingIOError:             pass         pid, status = os.waitpid(supervisor, os.WNOHANG)         if pid:             # The exit can race the preceding pipe read. Consume its explicit             # completion acknowledgement before accepting a normal exit code.             with contextlib.suppress(BlockingIOError):                 completed = completed or b"C" in os.read(read_fd, 4096)             if os.WIFEXITED(status) and completed:                 return os.WEXITSTATUS(status)             stop("supervisor_exit_unconfirmed", wait_status=status)         deadline = HEARTBEAT_DEADLINE if started else SCAN_DEADLINE + 10         age = time.monotonic() - last_heartbeat         if cancelled or os.getppid() != parent or age > deadline:             # Also fences a wedged fsync/receipt or process-identity probe. A             # missing receipt is unconfirmed execution, never safe local replay.             reason = "cancelled" if cancelled else (                 "parent_lost" if os.getppid() != parent else "heartbeat_timeout")             stop(reason, heartbeat_age_seconds=age)         time.sleep(0.1)   def main(argv):     budget, floor = int(argv[0]), int(argv[1])     marker, target, plan, mode = argv[2], argv[3], json.loads(argv[4]), argv[5]     if not positive_int(budget) or not nonnegative_int(floor) or not argv[6:]:         raise ValueError("positive build budget, nonnegative floor, and command required")     if len(marker) > 160 or not marker.endswith("_DISK_BUDGET:") or any(c.isspace() for c in marker):         raise ValueError("invalid disk receipt marker")     paths, outputs = plan["probe"], plan["output"]     if any(not isinstance(items, list) or not items or len(items) > 64 for items in (paths, outputs)):         raise ValueError("invalid disk path plan")     if any(not isinstance(path, str) or not os.path.isabs(path) for path in paths + outputs + [target]):         raise ValueError("disk paths must be absolute")     receipt_path = plan.get("receipt")     if receipt_path is not None and (not isinstance(receipt_path, str) or not os.path.isabs(receipt_path)):         raise ValueError("disk receipt path must be absolute")     timeout_seconds = plan.get("timeout_seconds", 0)     if type(timeout_seconds) is not int or not 0 <= timeout_seconds <= (1 << 63) - 1:         raise ValueError("invalid execution deadline")     receipt_initialized = plan.get("receipt_initialized", False)     if type(receipt_initialized) is not bool:         raise ValueError("invalid receipt initialization mode")     require_incremental_tracking = plan.get("require_incremental_tracking", False)     if type(require_incremental_tracking) is not bool:         raise ValueError("invalid required output tracking mode")     return run_guarded(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, argv[6:], mode, timeout_seconds, receipt_initialized, plan.get("warm_policy"), require_incremental_tracking, plan.get("cargo_cache"), plan.get("cargo_cache_required", False))   if __name__ == "__main__":     os._exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))  10737418240 10737418240 RCH_EXTERNAL_DEADLINE:c3f5dc2d-011a-4be1-be3d-8bd7d0face03_DISK_BUDGET: 775253 python3 -I -S -c """Worker-local build disk admission and early termination (Python stdlib only).  Embedded by RCH so the SSH execution path cannot depend on an upgraded worker binary. Polling is not a filesystem quota: fast bursts can overrun an interval, and process groups do not contain a child deliberately starting another session. Use the existing cgroup/service scope for that stronger process containment. No output is removed here. Reservations are retired only after their group is observed dead, never by TTL, PID reuse, or an incomplete directory scan. """  import concurrent.futures import contextlib import errno import fcntl import hashlib import json import os import signal import stat import subprocess import sys import tempfile import time import uuid   def timing_scale():     """RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT (whole percent, set by the RCH client from RCH_TIMING_SCALE): 100..1000, bad value -> 1.0."""     raw = os.environ.get("RCH_TIMING_SCALE_PCT", "")     return int(raw) / 100.0 if raw.isascii() and raw.isdigit() and raw[0] != "0" and 100 <= int(raw) <= 1000 else 1.0   # Local patch 6: one multiplier for every fixed DEADLINE below (intervals and poll rates are not scaled). SCALE = timing_scale() POLL_SECONDS = 0.25 # Local patch 4 (fleksprime owner, 5 Nov 2026): was 1.0. Under CPU starvation (load ~40 on 18 cores) one statvfs # sample in this Python monitor took >1 s and RCH killed a healthy build with E330 'free-space telemetry deadline'. # Samples are still requested every POLL_SECONDS; only the declare-the-monitor-dead deadline is longer. FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE = 5.0 * SCALE # Local patch 3 (fleksprime owner, 5 Nov 2026): was 2.0. Each full walk of a large cargo target cost 1-2 s of CPU per # build; the per-build output check now runs every 10 s. Free-space sampling (POLL_SECONDS) is unchanged. SCAN_INTERVAL = 10.0 SCAN_DEADLINE = 30.0 * SCALE HEARTBEAT_DEADLINE = 3.0 * SCALE LOCK_DEADLINE = 2.0 * SCALE ADMIT_DEADLINE = 5.0 * SCALE EXIT_BUDGET = 120 MAX_LEDGER_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024 MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES = 8192 # Extra learning metadata is bounded independently of the existing output # scan. Oversized or changing trees still receive the ordinary disk guards; # they simply cannot attest a smaller, pool-dependent reservation. MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES = 100_000 # A worker's shared Cargo cache can hold 600,000 entries, and one cold walk took # 30 seconds on an idle worker. Every build walks its outputs each SCAN_INTERVAL # under SCAN_DEADLINE, so walking a cache that size stops builds on a busy worker # and exhausts the entries above. Past this bound the cache keeps its reservation # and floor protection, but its existing and new bytes are not charged to a build. MAX_WALKED_CACHE_ENTRIES = 25_000 DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX = "_DISK_BUDGET:"   class BudgetFailure(Exception):     def __init__(self, reason, free=0, growth=0, detail="", required_growth_bytes=None): positive_int(value):     return type(value) is int and 0 < value <= (1 << 63) - 1   def nonnegative_int(value):     return type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= (1 << 63) - 1   def growth_buffer(budget):     return min(256 * 1024 * 1024, max(1, budget // 16))   def validate_warm_policy(policy, budget):     if policy is None:         return      def digest(value):         return (isinstance(value, list) and len(value) == 32                 and all(type(byte) is int and 0 <= byte <= 255 for byte in value)                 and any(value))      def pool(value):         return (isinstance(value, dict)                 and set(value) == {"identity", "state", "retained_bytes"}                 and digest(value["identity"]) and digest(value["state"])                 and positive_int(value["retained_bytes"]))      if (not positive_int(budget)             or not isinstance(policy, dict)             or set(policy) != {"cold_growth_bytes", "pools"}             or not BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="duplicate warm disk pool identity")   def boot_identity():     if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):         with open("/proc/self/status", encoding="ascii") as stream:             namespace = [line.split()[1:] for line in stream if line.startswith("NSpid:")]         # A foreign procfs cannot prove that a recorded process group is gone.         if namespace != [[str(os.getpid())]]:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unverified process namespace")         with open("/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id", encoding="ascii") as stream:             boot = stream.read().strip()     elif sys.platform == "darwin":         boot = subprocess.check_output(             ["sysctl", "-n", "kern.bootsessionuuid"], raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid kernel boot identity")     return boot   def linux_process(path):     with open(path, encoding="utf-8", errors="strict") as stream:         data = stream.read(8192)     prefix, sep, tail = data.rpartition(") ")     fields = tail.split()     if not sep or len(fields) < 20:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable",     }   def process_snapshot(heartbeat=lambda: None):     result = {}     if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):         with os.scandir("/proc") as entries:             for entry in entries:                 # A large, progressing census is not a wedged kernel read.                 # Keep the guardian armed between records; any individual                 # scandir/read stall still receives no heartbeat and is fenced.                 heartbeat()                 if not entry.name.isdecimal():                     continue                 try:                     record = linux_process(entry.path + "/stat")                     result[record["pid"]] = record                     # A dead main thread does not prove that its sibling                     # threads stopped writing. Linux retains their group.                     if record["state"] in ("Z", "X"):                         with os.scandir(entry.path + "/task") as tasks:                             for task in tasks:                                 heartbeat()                                 try:                                     thread = linux_process(task.path + "/stat")                                     result[thread["pid"]] = thread                                 except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                                     pass                 except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                     # An opened procfs record can return ESRCH when its task                     # exits before read, rather than ENOENT from open.                     pass     elif sys.platform == "darwin":         census = subprocess.Popen(             ["ps", "-axo", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,stat="], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, text=True         )         try:             output, _ = census.communicate(timeout=1)         except BaseException:             census.kill()             census.communicate()             raise         if census.returncode:             raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(census.returncode, census.args, output)         for line in output.splitlines():             heartbeat()             pid, parent, group, state = line.split()             # This exact child has been reaped above. Its census entry is not             # an outstanding build writer, even though ps observed itself.             if int(pid) == census.pid:                 continue             result[int(pid)] = {                 "pid": int(pid), "parent": int(parent), "group": int(group),                 "state": state[0], "start": None,             }     else:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="process census is unavailable")     if os.getpid() not in result:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="process census omitted supervisor")     return result   def darwin_leader_start(group, boot):     # Native witness rejects dead/zombie leaders and rechecks birth and group     # membership. A whole-machine ps census adds no owner identity evidence.     witness = subprocess.check_output(         ["rch-wkr", "process-identity", str(group)], timeout=1, text=True     ).split()     if len(witness) != 3 or witness[0] != boot or int(witness[2]) != group:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unverified Darwin group identity")     return witness[1]   def leader_start(group, snapshot):     record = snapshot.get(group)     if record is None or record["state"] in ("Z", "X"):         raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader disappeared")     if sys.platform == "darwin":         # ps display timestamps are too coarse to fence PID reuse. Live group membership wins.     return record["boot"] == boot and record["group"] in groups   def filesystem_sample(paths):     samples = {}     for path in paths:         # Bind capacity to the same directory as its device identity. Separate         # path-based stat/statvfs calls can combine different filesystems when         # a symlink or mount path changes between the two operations.         descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_CLOEXEC)         try:             device = os.fstat(descriptor).st_dev             info = os.fstatvfs(descriptor)         finally:             os.close(descriptor)         available = info.f_bavail * info.f_frsize         samples[device] = min(samples.get(device, available), available)     return samples   def output_bindings(paths):     identities = {}     for path in paths:         info = os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False)         if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output root is not a physical directory")         identities[path] = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)     return identities   def verify_output_bindings(identities):     if output_bindings(identities) != identities:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output root changed during execution")   def validate_cargo_cache_plan(plan, required=False):     if type(required) is not bool:         raise            "monitor_unavailable",             detail="Cargo cache scope is unresolved; use a literal absolute CARGO_HOME and supported env prefix",         )   def prepare_cargo_cache_roots(plan):     """Bind the worker's isolated home and only Cargo's known cache redirects.      The transfer shell resolves RCH_CH_BASE before client env forwarding and     before cd. Never expand a client path or walk arbitrary cache symlinks.     At most six individual directories are created before admission; missing     ancestor trees are refused rather than seeded outside the reservation.     """     if plan is None:         return [], {}     validate_cargo_cache_plan(plan)     base = os.environ.get("RCH_CH_BASE", "")     if (not os.path.isabs(base) or os.path.realpath(base) != base             or not os.path.isdir(base)):         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker Cargo cache base is not a resolved physical directory")     homes = [os.path.join(base, plan["name"])]     if plan["additional_home"] is not None:         homes.append(plan["additional_home"])     aliases = {}     for home in dict.fromkeys(homes):         for logical in (home, os.path.join(home, "registry"), os.path.join(home, "git")):             physical = os.path.realpath(logical)             if not os.path.isdir(os.path.dirname(physical)):                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache parent must already exist before admission")             try:                 os.mkdir(physical)             except FileExistsError:                 pass             if not stat.S_ISDIR(os.stat(physical, follow_symlinks=False).st_mode):                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache output root is not a physical directory")             aliases[logical] = physical     verify_cargo_cache_bindings(aliases)     return list(dict.fromkeys(aliases.values())), aliases   def cache_walk_is_bounded(roots):     """Stop counting at the bound, so a large cache costs one small walk per build."""     entries, visited, pending = 0, set(), list(roots)     while pending:         path = pending.pop()         # Roots nest, and each directory's entries count only once.         if path in visited:             continue         visited.add(path)         with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError, NotADirectoryError), os.scandir(path) as listing:             for entry in listing:                 entries += 1                 if entries > MAX_WALKED_CACHE_ENTRIES:                     return False                 if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):                     pending.append(entry.path)     return True   def verify_cargo_cache_bindings(aliases):     for logical, physical in aliases.items():         if os.path.realpath(logical) != physical:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="Cargo cache redirect changed during execution")   class OutputScan:     def __init__(self, total, records=None, pool_state=None, pool_bytes=0, added=None):         self.total, self.records = total, records         self.pool_state, self.pool_bytes = pool_state, pool_bytes         self.added = added   def output_signature(info):     # A modified/reused inode cannot retain its old allocation credit. Merely     # subtracting its starting size would miss replacement output after cleanup.     # Atime is deliberately absent: reading a retained artifact is not a write.     return (info.st_blocks * 512, info.st_mode, info.st_size,             info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns)   def measure_outputs(paths, target=None, baseline_records=None):     """Allocated bytes and bounded inode evidence; never follow symlinks.      Scan the target first so overlapping source/output roots cannot remove     entries from its fingerprint through cross-root inode deduplication. Pool     names and signatures are sorted only after collecting a bounded snapshot.     Runtime additions stream against the immutable baseline even after the     optional current snapshot exceeds its bound or loses fingerprint evidence.     """     total, pool_bytes, seen = 0, 0, set()     added = 0 if baseline_records is not None else None     records, pool_entries = {}, []     devices = {identity[0] for identity in output_bindings(paths).values()}      def incomplete():         nonlocal records, pool_entries         records, pool_entries = None, None      def visit(name, parent=None, relative=None):         nonlocal total, pool_bytes, added         try:             info = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)             identity = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)             signature = output_signature(info)             if relative is not None and pool_entries is not None:                 if len(pool_entries) >= MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES:                     incomplete()                 else:                     pool_entries.append((relative, *identity, *signature))             if identity in seen:                 return             if info.st_dev not in devices:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unmonitored filesystem inside output root")             seen.add(identity)             total += signature[0]             if added is not None and baseline_records.get(identity) != signature:                 added += signature[0]             if relative is not None:                 pool_bytes += signature[0]             if records is not None:                 if len(records) >= MAX_INCREMENTAL_ENTRIES:                     incomplete()                 else:                     records[identity] = signature             if stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):                 descriptor = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)                 try:                     current = os.fstat(descriptor)                     if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != identity:                         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output directory changed during scan")                     if output_signature(current) != signature:                         incomplete()                     with os.scandir(descriptor) as entries:                         for entry in entries:                             child = None if relative is None else relative + "/" + entry.name                             visit(entry.name, descriptor, child)                     # A tree that changed while enumerated cannot certify an                     # exact retained-pool proof, even if its byte total fits.                     if output_signature(os.fstat(descriptor)) != signature:                         incomplete()                 finally:                     os.close(descriptor)         except OSError as error:             # Cargo renames and removes intermediates while compiling. A             # disappearing managed root is separately rejected by its binding.             if error.errno not in (errno.ENOENT, errno.ENOTDIR, errno.ELOOP):                 raise             incomplete()     try:         ordered = paths if target is None else list(dict.fromkeys([target] + paths))         for path in ordered:             visit(path, relative="." if path == target else None)         pool_state = None         if target is not None and pool_entries is not None:             fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(b"rch-pool-state-v1\0")             for entry in sorted(pool_entries):                 fingerprint.update(json.dumps(entry, separators=(",", ":")).encode("ascii"))                 fingerprint.update(b"\n")             pool_state = list(fingerprint.digest())         return OutputScan(total, records, pool_state, pool_bytes, added)     finally:         # Recursion captures this function's own cell. Break that cycle on         # success AND failure so every scan releases its inode table promptly,         # instead of accumulating entire trees until cyclic GC runs.         visit = None   def private_regular(descriptor):     info = os.fstat(descriptor)     if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unsafe worker accounting file")   def atomic_json(path, value):     descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".disk-", dir=os.path.dirname(path))     with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:         json.dump(value, stream, separators=(",", ":"))         stream.flush()         os.fsync(stream.fileno())     os.replace(temporary, path)   class Registry:     def __init__(self, root=None):         self.root = root or "/var/tmp/rch-build-disk-" + str(os.getuid())         try:             os.mkdir(self.root, 0o700)         except FileExistsError:             pass         info = os.stat(self.root, follow_symlinks=False)         if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="unsafe worker accounting directory")      @contextlib.contextmanager     def locked(self):         descriptor = os.open(self.root + "/lock", os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)         try:             private_regular(descriptor)             deadline = time.monotonic() + LOCK_DEADLINE             while True:                 try:                     fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)                     break                 except BlockingIOError:                     if time.monotonic() >= deadline:                         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker reservation lock deadline")                     time.sleep(0.025)             yield         finally:             os.close(descriptor)      def read(self):         try:             descriptor = os.open(self.root + "/ledger.json", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)         except FileNotFoundError:             return []         with os.fdopen(descriptor, "r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:             private_regular(stream.fileno())             source = stream.read(MAX_LEDGER_BYTES + 1)             if len(source) > MAX_LEDGER_BYTES:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker disk ledger exceeded size limit")             value = json.loads(source)         if not isinstance(value, isinstance(entries, list) or len(entries) > 4096:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid worker disk ledger")         for entry in entries:             if (                 not isinstance(entry, dict) or not positive_int(entry.get("group"))                 or entry["group"] <= 1 or not positive_int(entry.get("budget"))                 or not isinstance(entry.get("boot"), str)                 or str(uuid.UUID(entry["boot"])) != entry["boot"]             ):                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid worker disk reservation")         return entries      def admit(self, monitor):         # Counting every reservation is conservative and avoids a whole-host         # process census on the common path. Retire records only when capacity         # or the ledger limit requires it, using the authoritative census.         with self.locked():             records = self.read()             free = monitor.sample()             if set(free) != monitor.devices:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="filesystem changed before admission")              def fits():                 return all(available >= max([monitor.floor] + [entry["floor"] for entry in records if device in entry["devices"]])                            + monitor.budget + sum(entry["budget"] for entry in records if device in entry["devices"])                            for device, available in free.items())              if len(records) >= 4096 or not fits():                 groups = alive_groups(process_snapshot())                 records = [entry for entry in records if reservation_alive(entry, monitor.boot, groups)]                 free = monitor.sample()                 if set(free) != monitor.devices:                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="filesystem changed before admission")             if len(records) >= 4096:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="worker disk ledger reservation limit")             floors = {}             for device, available in free.items():                 same_device = [entry for entry in records if device in entry["devices"]]                 floor = max([monitor.floor] + [entry["floor"] for entry in same_device])                 required = floor + monitor.budget + sum(entry["budget"] for entry in same_device)                 if available < required:                     raise BudgetFailure("admission", available, detail="free space cannot cover active build reservations and protected floor")                 floors[device] = floor             records.append({                 "boot": monitor.boot, "group": monitor.group, "leader_start": monitor.start,                 "devices": sorted(monitor.devices), "budget": monitor.budget, "floor": monitor.floor,             })             atomic_json(self.root + "/ledger.json", {"version": 1, "entries": records})             return free, floors   class Monitor:     def __init__(self, budget, floor, target, paths, outputs=None, cargo_cache=None):         self.budget, self.floor = budget, floor         self.target = target         self.boot, self.group = boot_identity(), os.getpgrp()         cache_outputs, self.cargo_cache_bindings = prepare_cargo_cache_roots(cargo_cache)         walked_cache = cache_outputs if cache_walk_is_bounded(cache_outputs) else []         self.outputs = list(dict.fromkeys([target] + (outputs or []) + walked_cache))         # Every managed output volume needs both a reservation and ongoing         # floor protection, even when a caller omitted it from its probe list.         self.paths = list(dict.fromkeys(paths + [target] + self.outputs + cache_outputs))         self.bindings = output_bindings(self.outputs)         if self.group <= 1:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="build has no isolated process group")         snapshot = process_snapshot()         self.start = leader_start(self.group, snapshot)         # Keep the command's enclosing shells alive on successful completion.         self.controls = set()         parent = os.getpid()         while parent in snapshot and snapshot[parent]["group"] == self.group:             self.controls.add(parent)             parent = snapshot[parent]["parent"]         self.parent = os.getppid()         self.devices = set(self.sample())         identity = ["rch-pool-identity-v1", os.path.abspath(target), self.boot,                     *self.bindings[target]]
