self.baseline_records = initial.total, initial.records         self.baseline_pool = self.pool_observation(initial)         self.final_pool, self.admitted_pool = self.baseline_pool, None         self.incremental_reliable = initial.records is not None and self.baseline_pool is not None         self.peak_added, self.unproven_baseline, self.strict_tracking = 0, 0, False         self.peak_output, self.peak_growth = self.baseline, 0         self.free = {}         self.free_start, self.free_min = {}, {}         self.floors = {device: floor for device in self.devices}         self.previous = None      def sample(self):         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         samples = filesystem_sample(self.paths)         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         return samples      def scan(self):         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         result = measure_outputs(self.outputs, self.target, getattr(self, "baseline_records", None))         verify_output_bindings(self.bindings)         verify_cargo_cache_bindings(getattr(self, "cargo_cache_bindings", {}))         return result      def pool_observation(self, scan):         if scan.pool_state is None:             return None         return {"identity": self.pool_identity, "state": scan.pool_state,                 "retained_bytes": scan.pool_bytes}      def require_runtime_tracking(self, required):         if type(required) is not bool:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="invalid required output tracking mode")         if required and self.baseline_records is None:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="bounded initial output tracking is unavailable")         self.strict_tracking = self.strict_tracking or required      def require_warm_pool(self, policy):         validate_warm_policy(policy, self.budget)         if policy is None:             return         if self.baseline_pool is None or self.baseline_pool not in policy["pools"]:             raise BudgetFailure(                 "cold_required", detail="retained output pool no longer matches its warm reservation",                 required_growth_bytes=policy["cold_growth_bytes"],             )         # Only the attested target pool has already been funded. Source sync,         # caches, and other roots cannot borrow its retained-artifact credit.         # Keep a growth buffer available before installing the reservation;         # a changed source baseline must request cold admission, not silently         # consume the whole allowance that was selected for this build.         unproven = self.baseline - self.baseline_pool["retained_bytes"]         if unproven + growth_buffer(self.budget) >= self.budget:             raise BudgetFailure(                 "cold_required",                 detail=f"unproven output baseline ({unproven} bytes) leaves insufficient warm growth allowance",                 required_growth_bytes=policy["cold_growth_bytes"],             )         self.unproven_baseline = unproven         self.strict_tracking = True         self.peak_growth = max(self.peak_growth, unproven)         # Admission proof survives a later loss of optional learning metadata.         # Losing that metadata must not turn confirmed completion into replay.         self.admitted_pool = self.baseline_pool      def admit(self):         self.free, self.floors = Registry().admit(self)         self.free_start, self.free_min = dict(self.free), dict(self.free)         self.previous = (time.monotonic(), self.free)      def observe_free(self, samples, now):         if set(samples) != self.devices:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="monitored filesystem identity changed")         for device, free in samples.items():             self.free_min[device] = min(self.free_min.get(device, free), free)             base = growth_buffer(self.budget)             margin = base             if self.previous is not None:                 previous_time, previous_samples = self.previous                 rate = max(0, previous_samples[device] - free) / max(now - previous_time, 0.001)                 margin = max(base, int(rate * 2))             if free <= self.floors[device] + margin:                 raise BudgetFailure("free_floor", free, self.peak_growth, "protected floor or observed write-rate buffer reached")         self.free, self.previous = samples, (now, samples)      def observe_output(self, scan):         self.peak_output = max(self.peak_output, scan.total)         net_growth = max(0, scan.total - self.baseline)         self.final_pool = self.pool_observation(scan)         if scan.records is None or self.final_pool is None:             self.incremental_reliable = False         added = scan.added         if added is None and self.baseline_records is not None and scan.records is not None:             added = sum(signature[0] for identity, signature in scan.records.items()                         if self.baseline_records.get(identity) != signature)         if added is None and self.strict_tracking:             # Never turn an already-small reservation into net-only tracking.             # Unknown attribution receives no retained credit. Confirmed             # completion remains possible if this conservative charge fits.             self.incremental_reliable = False             added = scan.total         if added is not None:             self.peak_added = max(self.peak_added, added)             # Old-artifact cleanup cannot erase the new output's reservation             # charge. Other jobs' overlapping output may be charged too, never             # divided into unsupported per-writer attribution.         # Preserve the unproven baseline charge even if a later scan loses         # bounded evidence. Changed unproven inodes may be charged once at         # admission and again as new output; that conservative aggregate can         # exceed the largest simultaneously allocated output snapshot.         self.peak_growth = max(             self.peak_growth, self.unproven_baseline + max(net_growth, self.peak_added)         )         if self.peak_growth > self.budget:             raise BudgetFailure("growth", min(self.free.values(), default=0), self.peak_growth, "allocated build output exceeded its reservation")      def check_owner(self):         if os.getppid() != self.parent:             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="disk guardian disappeared")         if sys.platform == "darwin":             if darwin_leader_start(self.group, self.boot) != self.start:                 raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader changed")             return         if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):             try:                 snapshot = {self.group: linux_process("/proc/" + str(self.group) + "/stat")}             except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):                 snapshot = {}         else:             snapshot = process_snapshot()         if leader_start(self.group, snapshot) != self.start:             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="build group leader changed")   def kill_owned_group(group):     # Our own live membership prevents PGID reuse, even after the leader died     # or process telemetry failed. Never signal an arbitrary ledger PID/group.     if group <= 1 or os.getpgrp() != group:         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="lost own build process group")     os.killpg(group, signal.SIGKILL)   def receipts_for_failure(marker, failure, budget, floor, started, monitor):     receipt = {         "reason": failure.reason, "free_bytes": failure.free, "growth_bytes": failure.growth,         "max_growth_bytes": budget, "min_free_bytes": floor, "started": started,         "detail": failure.detail[:240],         "peak_output_bytes": monitor.peak_output if monitor is not None else None,     }     if failure.reason == "cold_required":         receipt["required_growth_bytes"] = failure.required_growth_bytes     receipts = [(marker, receipt)]     if started and monitor is not None:         receipts.append(usage_receipt(marker, monitor, False))     return receipts   def usage_receipt(marker, monitor, complete, exit_code=None):     incremental = None     if monitor.incremental_reliable:         incremental = {             "version": 1, "baseline_output_bytes": monitor.baseline,             "peak_added_output_bytes": monitor.peak_added,             "peak_free_drop_bytes": sum(max(0, start - monitor.free_min[device])                                         for device, start in monitor.free_start.items()),             "baseline_pool": monitor.baseline_pool, "final_pool": monitor.final_pool,         }     return (marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)] + "_DISK_USAGE:", {         "max_growth_bytes": monitor.budget, "min_free_bytes": monitor.floor,         "peak_output_bytes": monitor.peak_output, "peak_growth_bytes": monitor.peak_growth,         "incremental": incremental, "admitted_pool": monitor.admitted_pool,         "complete": complete, "exit_code": exit_code,     })   def receipt_text(receipts):     return "".join(marker + (json.dumps(value, separators=(",", ":")) if value is not None else "") + "\n" for marker, value in receipts)   def persist_receipts(path, receipts):     descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".disk-receipt-", dir=os.path.dirname(path))     with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:         stream.write(receipt_text(receipts))         stream.flush()         os.fsync(stream.fileno())     os.replace(temporary, path)   def emit_receipts(receipts):     # This is a supplement to the durable receipt. Never wait for a noisy     # workload's full stderr before stopping its writers; restore fd flags.     flags = fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_GETFL)     try:         fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_SETFL, flags | os.O_NONBLOCK)         with contextlib.suppress(OSError):             os.write(2, ("\n" + receipt_text(receipts)).encode())     finally:         with contextlib.suppress(OSError):             fcntl.fcntl(2, fcntl.F_SETFL, flags)   def bounded_result(future, deadline, heartbeat):     while not future.done():         heartbeat()         if time.monotonic() >= deadline:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="disk probe or scan deadline reached")         time.sleep(0.025)     return future.result()   def finish_descendants(monitor, heartbeat):     deadline = None     while True:         heartbeat()         monitor.check_owner()         snapshot = process_snapshot(heartbeat)         descendants = [record for record in snapshot.values()                        if record["group"] == monitor.group and record["pid"] not in monitor.controls                        and record["state"] not in ("Z", "X")]         if not descendants:             return         # A progressing first census can be slow on a busy worker. Give the         # writers their bounded exit window after discovering them, rather         # than expiring it before the first signal. The guardian still fences         # a stalled census, and completion still requires an empty census.         if deadline is None:             deadline = time.monotonic() + 2         for record in descendants:             # Pin the process on Linux so a recycled numeric PID cannot receive             # a signal between membership inspection and signal delivery.             with contextlib.suppress(ProcessLookupError):                 if hasattr(os, "pidfd_open") and hasattr(signal, "pidfd_send_signal"):                     descriptor = os.pidfd_open(record["pid"])                     try:                         if os.getpgid(record["pid"]) == monitor.group:                             signal.pidfd_send_signal(descriptor, signal.SIGKILL)                     finally:                         os.close(descriptor)                 elif os.getpgid(record["pid"]) == monitor.group:                     # Native Unix cancellation already uses an owned group.                     # Without pidfd, stop that still-owned group rather than                     # risk signalling a recycled individual PID.                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="background writer cleanup requires whole-group cancellation")         if time.monotonic() >= deadline:             raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="background build writers did not stop")         time.sleep(0.025)   def supervise(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, heartbeat, timeout_seconds=0, receipt_initialized=False, warm_policy=None, require_incremental_tracking=False, cargo_cache=None, cargo_cache_required=False):     monitor = child = None     started = receipt_owned = False     executor = concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2)     try:         if receipt_path is not None:             if receipt_initialized:                 descriptor = os.open(receipt_path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)                 with os.fdopen(descriptor, "r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:                     private_regular(stream.fileno())                     if stream.read(MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES + 1) != marker + "\n":                         raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="disk receipt initialization does not match this attempt")             else:                 descriptor = os.open(receipt_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)                 os.close(descriptor)             receipt_owned = True         validate_warm_policy(warm_policy, budget)         validate_cargo_cache_plan(cargo_cache, cargo_cache_required)         monitor = bounded_result(             executor.submit(Monitor, budget, floor, target, paths, outputs, cargo_cache),             time.monotonic() + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat,         )         monitor.require_warm_pool(warm_policy)         monitor.require_runtime_tracking(require_incremental_tracking)         bounded_result(executor.submit(monitor.admit), time.monotonic() + ADMIT_DEADLINE, heartbeat)          def interrupted(signum, frame):             raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="supervisor received signal " + str(signum))          for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):             signal.signal(signum, interrupted)         # Signal delivery can interrupt Popen after the OS created its child,         # before Python returns it. That is possibly started, never a retryable         # admission refusal. A group kill does not require a returned child PID.         started = True         heartbeat(b"S")         execution_started = time.monotonic()         child = subprocess.Popen(command, close_fds=True)         free_future = executor.submit(monitor.sample)         free_started = time.monotonic()         scan_future = executor.submit(monitor.scan)         scan_started = free_started         next_scan = scan_started + SCAN_INTERVAL         while child.poll() is None:             heartbeat()             monitor.check_owner()             now = time.monotonic()             if timeout_seconds and now - execution_started >= timeout_seconds:                 raise BudgetFailure("deadline", detail="remote execution deadline reached")             if free_future.done():                 monitor.observe_free(free_future.result(), now)                 free_future = executor.submit(monitor.sample)                 free_started = now             elif now - free_started > FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE:                 raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="free-space telemetry deadline")             if scan_future is not None:                 if scan_future.done():                     monitor.observe_output(scan_future.result())                     scan_future = None                 elif now - scan_started > SCAN_DEADLINE:                     raise BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail="output scan deadline")             if scan_future is None and now >= next_scan:                 scan_future = executor.submit(monitor.scan)                 scan_started, next_scan = now, now + SCAN_INTERVAL             time.sleep(POLL_SECONDS)         finish_descendants(monitor, heartbeat)         monitor.observe_free(bounded_result(free_future, free_started + FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE, heartbeat), time.monotonic())         if scan_future is not None:             monitor.observe_output(bounded_result(scan_future, scan_started + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat))         monitor.observe_output(bounded_result(executor.submit(monitor.scan), time.monotonic() + SCAN_DEADLINE, heartbeat))         # The in-flight free-space sample can predate the command's last writes         # (or the entire command). A final output scan can also take seconds         # while unrelated jobs consume this filesystem. Only a NEW probe after         # writers stop and scanning finishes can attest the completion floor.         monitor.observe_free(bounded_result(             executor.submit(monitor.sample), time.monotonic() + FREE_SAMPLE_DEADLINE, heartbeat,         ), time.monotonic())         # Completion means the foreground command ended, remaining writers         # stopped, and final output and free-space checks passed. A nonzero exit         # remains a complete measurement; callers gate successful learning on         # that independent command status.         exit_code = child.returncode if child.returncode >= 0 else 128 - child.returncode         receipts = [usage_receipt(marker, monitor, True, exit_code)]         if receipt_owned:             persist_receipts(receipt_path, receipts)         emit_receipts(receipts)         return exit_code     except BaseException as error:         try:             failure = error if isinstance(error, BudgetFailure) else BudgetFailure("monitor_unavailable", detail=str(error))             if failure.reason == "deadline":                 receipts = [(marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)], None)]                 if monitor is not None:                     receipts.append(usage_receipt(marker, monitor, False))             else:                 receipts = receipts_for_failure(marker, failure, budget, floor, started, monitor)             # A failing filesystem can also wedge receipt fsync. Publish the             # bounded, nonblocking failure diagnostic first so the guardian's             # subsequent kill does not erase the reason. This is never a             # completion acknowledgement; durable success still comes first.             with contextlib.suppress(OSError):                 emit_receipts(receipts)             if receipt_owned:                 with contextlib.suppress(OSError):                     persist_receipts(receipt_path, receipts)         finally:             # A broken serializer, receipt writer, or diagnostic must never             # bypass the only cleanup boundary for a possibly started child.             if started:                 kill_owned_group(os.getpgrp())         return EXIT_BUDGET     finally:         # os._exit below terminates outstanding sampler threads too. Avoid         # cancel_futures (Python 3.9+) on workers with Python 3.8.         executor.shutdown(wait=False)   def run_guarded(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, mode, timeout_seconds=0, receipt_initialized=False, warm_policy=None, require_incremental_tracking=False, cargo_cache=None, cargo_cache_required=False):     if mode == "standalone":         os.setsid()     elif mode != "tracked":         raise ValueError("invalid build supervisor mode")     group, parent = os.getpgrp(), os.getppid()     read_fd, write_fd = os.pipe()     os.set_blocking(read_fd, False)     os.set_blocking(write_fd, False)     supervisor = os.fork()     if supervisor == 0:         os.close(read_fd)          def heartbeat(value=b"H"):             if os.getppid() != parent_guardian:                 raise BudgetFailure("interrupted", detail="disk guardian disappeared")             with contextlib.suppress(BlockingIOError):                 os.write(write_fd, value)          parent_guardian = os.getppid()         result = supervise(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, command, heartbeat, timeout_seconds, receipt_initialized, warm_policy, require_incremental_tracking, cargo_cache, cargo_cache_required)         os.write(write_fd, b"C")         os._exit(result)     os.close(write_fd)     cancelled, started, completed = False, False, False      def interrupted(signum, frame):         nonlocal cancelled         cancelled = True      def stop(reason, **detail):         # Diagnostics must not delay the existing kill fence. This separate         # marker never attests disk usage or successful command completion.         try:             emit_receipts([(marker[:-len(DISK_MARKER_SUFFIX)] + "_DISK_GUARDIAN:", {                 "reason": reason, "started": started, **detail,             })])         finally:             kill_owned_group(group)      for signum in (signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):         signal.signal(signum, interrupted)     last_heartbeat = time.monotonic()     while True:         try:             data = os.read(read_fd, 4096)             if data:                 started = started or b"S" in data                 completed = completed or b"C" in data                 last_heartbeat = time.monotonic()         except BlockingIOError:             pass         pid, status = os.waitpid(supervisor, os.WNOHANG)         if pid:             # The exit can race the preceding pipe read. Consume its explicit             # completion acknowledgement before accepting a normal exit code.             with contextlib.suppress(BlockingIOError):                 completed = completed or b"C" in os.read(read_fd, 4096)             if os.WIFEXITED(status) and completed:                 return os.WEXITSTATUS(status)             stop("supervisor_exit_unconfirmed", wait_status=status)         deadline = HEARTBEAT_DEADLINE if started else SCAN_DEADLINE + 10         age = time.monotonic() - last_heartbeat         if cancelled or os.getppid() != parent or age > deadline:             # Also fences a wedged fsync/receipt or process-identity probe. A             # missing receipt is unconfirmed execution, never safe local replay.             reason = "cancelled" if cancelled else (                 "parent_lost" if os.getppid() != parent else "heartbeat_timeout")             stop(reason, heartbeat_age_seconds=age)         time.sleep(0.1)   def main(argv):     budget, floor = int(argv[0]), int(argv[1])     marker, target, plan, mode = argv[2], argv[3], json.loads(argv[4]), argv[5]     if not positive_int(budget) or not nonnegative_int(floor) or not argv[6:]:         raise ValueError("positive build budget, nonnegative floor, and command required")     if len(marker) > 160 or not marker.endswith("_DISK_BUDGET:") or any(c.isspace() for c in marker):         raise ValueError("invalid disk receipt marker")     paths, outputs = plan["probe"], plan["output"]     if any(not isinstance(items, list) or not items or len(items) > 64 for items in (paths, outputs)):         raise ValueError("invalid disk path plan")     if any(not isinstance(path, str) or not os.path.isabs(path) for path in paths + outputs + [target]):         raise ValueError("disk paths must be absolute")     receipt_path = plan.get("receipt")     if receipt_path is not None and (not isinstance(receipt_path, str) or not os.path.isabs(receipt_path)):         raise ValueError("disk receipt path must be absolute")     timeout_seconds = plan.get("timeout_seconds", 0)     if type(timeout_seconds) is not int or not 0 <= timeout_seconds <= (1 << 63) - 1:         raise ValueError("invalid execution deadline")     receipt_initialized = plan.get("receipt_initialized", False)     if type(receipt_initialized) is not bool:         raise ValueError("invalid receipt initialization mode")     require_incremental_tracking = plan.get("require_incremental_tracking", False)     if type(require_incremental_tracking) is not bool:         raise ValueError("invalid required output tracking mode")     return run_guarded(budget, floor, marker, target, paths, outputs, receipt_path, argv[6:], mode, timeout_seconds, receipt_initialized, plan.get("warm_policy"), require_incremental_tracking, plan.get("cargo_cache"), plan.get("cargo_cache_required", False))   if __name__ == "__main__":     os._exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))  10737418240 10737418240 RCH_EXTERNAL_DEADLINE:c3f5dc2d-011a-4be1-be3d-8bd7d0face03_DISK_BUDGET:
